Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MRPRAN
Hangi Tablo için : FOSN
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : OTOSP1
Açýklama : Sipariþleri günlüðe parçala
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call AylikSiparisleriHaftaligaParcala()
	OTOSP1L.SafePack()
	i = 0
	Do
		i = i + 1
		if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRowCount()
		if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) = "H" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "AUF9A" and _
													trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "CVR1B" Then
			IlkPazartesi = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
			'KalanGunSayisi = DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi(IlkPazartesi, OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i))
			KalanGunSayisi = 6
			GunlukMiktar = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i), KalanGunSayisi))
			DagitilacakMiktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
			ret = OTOSP1L.ExportRowToXML(i)
			ret = Replace(ret, "<?xml version=""1.0""?>", "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""?>")
			Attrib = OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i)
			For j = 1 to KalanGunSayisi
				if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
				Miktar = GunlukMiktar
				if Kalan < Miktar Then Miktar = Kalan
				Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
				if j > 1 Then
					i = i + 1
					if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount Then
						OTOSP1L.AddRow
					Else
						OTOSP1L.InsertRow(i)
					End if
				End if
				OTOSP1L.SetCurrentRow(i)
				TrNum = OTOSP1L.TRNUM
				if j > 1 Then OTOSP1L.ImportRowFromXML i, ret
				OTOSP1L.TRNUM = TrNum
				OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "G"
				OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(IlkPazartesi, j-1)
				OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH
			Next
		End if
	Loop
End Sub
Function GetNextWeekAndDay(Tarih, Hafta, Gun)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	if d7Date.GetDOW() = Gun and Hafta > 0 Then Hafta = Hafta - 1
	d7Date.Increment Hafta * 7
	HaftaGun = d7Date.GetDOW()
	d7Date.Increment Gun - HaftaGun
	GetNextWeekAndDay = d7Date.GetRevDate
End Function
Function DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi(Tarih, Attrib)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	if Attrib = "A" Then
		d7Date.SetDate Tarih
		d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
		DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi = Doc.CDateToInt(d7Date.GetRevDate) - Doc.CDateToInt(Tarih) + 1
	Else
		DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi = 7
	End if
End Function
Sub AylikSiparisleriHaftaligaParcala()
	if Doc.IsBlank(OTOSP1L) Then Set OTOSP1L = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1L")
	OTOSP1L.SafePack()
	i = 0
	Do
		i = i + 1
		if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRowCount()
		if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) = "A" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "AUF9A" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "CVR1B" Then
			IlkPazartesi = ""
			QStr = "KOD='" & OTOSP1L.KOD(i) & "'&SEVK_EDILEN_FB='" & OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i) & "'&ATTRIB<>'A'"
			Set OSP1L = Doc.Select2("sdrgw4gs", "OTOSP1L", QStr, "<RTESTARIH", -1)
			if OSP1L.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				IlkPazartesi = GetNextWeekAndDay(Doc.StrDate_Increment(OSP1L.RTESTARIH(1), 1), 1, 1)
			End if
			AyinIlkPazartesiGunu = GetNextWeekAndDay(OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i), 1, 1)
			if AyinIlkPazartesiGunu > IlkPazartesi Then IlkPazartesi = AyinIlkPazartesiGunu

			KalanHaftaSayisi = AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi(IlkPazartesi)
			HaftalikMiktar = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i), KalanHaftaSayisi))
			DagitilacakMiktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
			ret = OTOSP1L.ExportRowToXML(i)
			ret = Replace(ret, "<?xml version=""1.0""?>", "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""?>")
			For j = 1 to KalanHaftaSayisi			
				if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
				Miktar = HaftalikMiktar
				if Kalan < Miktar Then Miktar = Kalan
				Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
				if j > 1 Then
					i = i + 1
					if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount Then
						OTOSP1L.AddRow
					Else
						OTOSP1L.InsertRow(i)
					End if
				End if
				OTOSP1L.SetCurrentRow(i)
				TrNum = OTOSP1L.TRNUM
				if j > 1 Then OTOSP1L.ImportRowFromXML i, ret
				OTOSP1L.TRNUM = TrNum
				OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "H"
				OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(IlkPazartesi, (j-1) * 7)
				OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH
			Next
		End if
	Loop
End Sub
Function KopyalanacakMi(AlanAdi)
	KopyalanacakMi = True
	if AlanAdi = "EVRAKNO" or AlanAdi = "TRNUM" or AlanAdi = "SRNUM" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00_") > 0 _
			or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_CARI00_") > 0 Then
		KopyalanacakMi = False
	End if
End Function
Function AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi(Tarih)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	KalanGun = Doc.CDateToInt(d7Date.GetRevDate) - Doc.CDateToInt(Tarih) + 1
	AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(KalanGun / 7)
End Function

Makro Kodu : KALIP-YM

Açýklama : Ay Sonu Yarýmamul Kalýp Ýþlemleri
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dder4444ffgt", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='KIM'&ALLOC_MPSEVNO<>'          '", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem yapýlacak MPS bulunamadý.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Else
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Ay sonu kalýp yarýmamul iþleminin baþlatýlmasýný onaylýyor musunuz?", "Freedom", MB_YESNO)
		if k<>6 Then Exit Sub
	End if
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Doc.BuGun
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	Tarih = InputBox("Ýlgili ayýn son gününü giriniz", "Freedom", d7Date.GetNorDate)
	if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Exit Sub

	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	Tarih = d7Date.GetNorDate
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	if d7Date.GetNorDate <> Tarih Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ayýn Son Gününü Girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	S01.GroupBy("ALLOC_MPSEVNO")
	Call YMUretimFisiniYarat(S01, Tarih)
	Call MPSIcinTransferYap()
End Sub
Sub YMUretimFisiniYarat(S01, Tarih)
	if trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then Doc.New_Voucher
	STOK20E.AMBCODE = "KYM"
	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "KIM"
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "KIM"
	STOK20E.TARIH = Tarih

	STOK20T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK20T.MPSKAPATMAENGEL = "E"
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand "RECETE_HESAPLA_MPSTRANSFERININ_TUMUNU_KULLAN"
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Sub MPSIcinTransferYap()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")	
	Set S83E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set S83T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S83E.AMBCODE = STOK20E.AMBCODE
	S83E.TARGETAMBCODE = "KIM"
	S83E.TARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(STOK20E.TARIH, 1)
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		IsNo = GetJobNo(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
		if trim(IsNo) <> "" Then
			satirNo = S83T.AddRow()
			S83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S83T.MPSNO = STOK20T.MPSNO(i)
			S83T.JOBNO = IsNo
			S83T.KOD = STOK20T.KOD(i)
			S83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S83T.SERINO = STOK20T.SERINO(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK20T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function GetJobNo(MPSNo)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sde34333", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetJobNo = M10T.JOBNO
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KALIP1

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK68
Açýklama : Kalýp Malzemelerini MPS 'e rezerve et
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set TRLIST = Doc.Select1("ddwefaf", "STOK01", "VRI_VR_R1<>'" & Space(24) & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '", "", -1)
	if TRLIST.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")
	Set S83E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set S83T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Set ff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	TRLIST.Sort("AMBCODE+VRI_VR_R1")
	For i = 1 to TRLIST.GetRowCount()	

		JobNo = GetMPSJobNo(Doc2, TRLIST.VRI_VR_R1(i), TRLIST.KOD(i), TRLIST.SF_MIKTAR(i))

		if trim(JobNo) = "" and Left(TRLIST.VRI_VR_R1(i), 1) = "P" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(TRLIST.VRI_VR_R1(i)) & " stok koduna ait MPS bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		End if
		If trim(JobNo) <> "" Then
			if TRLIST.AMBCODE(i) <> EskiAmbCode Then
				if trim(S83E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
					Doc1.Save_Voucher
				End if
				Doc1.New_Voucher()
				S83E.AMBCODE = TRLIST.AMBCODE(i)
				S83E.TARGETAMBCODE = TRLIST.AMBCODE(i)
			End if
			EskiAmbCode = TRLIST.AMBCODE(i)
			satirNo = S83T.AddRow()
			S83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S83T.MPSNO = Left(JobNo, 10)
			S83T.JOBNO = JobNo
			S83T.KOD = TRLIST.KOD(i)
			S83T.LOTNUMBER = TRLIST.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S83T.SERINO = TRLIST.SERINO(i)
			S83T.FOKOD = TRLIST.FOKOD(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = TRLIST.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = TRLIST.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = TRLIST.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = TRLIST.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = TRLIST.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = TRLIST.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S83T.LOCATION1 = TRLIST.LOCATION1(i)
			S83T.LOCATION2 = TRLIST.LOCATION2(i)
			S83T.LOCATION3 = TRLIST.LOCATION3(i)
			S83T.LOCATION4 = TRLIST.LOCATION4(i)
			S83T.SF_MIKTAR = TRLIST.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S83T.SF_SF_UNIT = TRLIST.SF_UNIT(i)
		End if

	Next
	if trim(S83E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
		if nret = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Kesildi", "", MB_OK
		End if
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub
Function GetMPSJobNo(Doc1, Varyant, HammaddeKodu, Miktar)
	GetMPSJobNo = ""

	MPSNo = GetFasonMPSNo(Varyant)
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" Then Exit Function
	Set MT = Doc1.Select1("fasf3fff", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & HammaddeKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set MT = Doc1.Select1("fasf3fff", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
		GetMPSJobNo = MT.JOBNO(1)
	Else
		GetMPSJobNo = MT.JOBNO(1)
	End if
End Function
Function GetMPSJobNo1(Doc1, Varyant, HammaddeKodu, Miktar)
	GetMPSJobNo = ""
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")

	MPSNo = GetFasonMPSNo(Varyant)
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" Then Exit Function
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSNo
	Set MT = Doc1.Select2("fasf3fff", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & HammaddeKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		satirNo = M10T.AddRow()
	Else
		satirNo = Doc.CDbl(MT.SRNUM(1))
	End if

	M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE = "H"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = HammaddeKodu
	M10T.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0 + Miktar
	GetMPSJobNo = M10T.JOBNO

	Doc1.Save_Voucher

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function GetFasonMPSNo(Varyant)
	GetFasonMPSNo = ""
	if trim(Varyant) = "" Then Exit Function
	Set M10E = Doc.Select1("fdsef34bfff", "MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU='" & Varyant & "'&HAVUZKODU='AL'", "", -1)
	if M10E.GetRowCount() <> 1 Then Exit Function
	GetFasonMPSNo = M10E.EVRAKNO(1)
End Function

Sub Makro1() 
	 
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)

	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = "ÝSTENÝLEN RAPOR FORMATI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,1).Value = "TEZGAH GRUP KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,2).Value = "TEZGAH ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,3).Value = "STOK KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,4).Value = "STOK ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,5).Value = "HAFTA1"
	Sh1.Cells(3,6).Value = "HAFTA2"
	Sh1.Cells(3,7).Value = "HAFTA3"
	Sh1.Cells(3,8).Value = "HAFTA4"
	Sh1.Cells(3,9).Value = "HAFTA5"
	Sh1.Cells(3,10).Value = "HAFTA6"
	Sh1.Cells(3,11).Value = "ÝÞ EMÝRÝ"

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(3,1),Sh1.Cells(3,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge

	Excel.Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 19
	Excel.Columns(2).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(3).ColumnWidth = 22
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 50
	Excel.Columns(5).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(6).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(7).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(8).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(9).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(10).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(11).ColumnWidth = 20

	HAFTA1 = 0
	HAFTA2 = 0
	HAFTA3 = 0
	HAFTA4 = 0
	HAFTA5 = 0
	HAFTA6 = 0

	satir = 4
	Set mpstarihi=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	Set bugun=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	bos = ""
	mpsno = ""

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1g("sda23","MMPS10T","ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"'","","R_KAYNAKKODU+R_MAMULKODU+R_LATEST_B_TARIH",-1)
	if M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for ii = 1 to M10T.GetRecCount()

'		if ii = 100 then 
'			msgbox buhafta &"---"& hafta
'			exit for
'		End if

			bugun.SetDate(doc.bugun)
			buhafta = bugun.GetWOY_ISO()

			mpstarihi.SetDate(M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(ii))
			hafta = mpstarihi.GetWOY_ISO()
			mpsmiktari = M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(ii)

			tezgahkodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(ii)
			tezgahadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgk1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_1",tezgahkodu,"")

			stokkodu = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(ii)
			stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")

			fark = hafta - buhafta
			if fark <= 0 then
				fark = 0
				mpsno = mpsno &","&M10T.EVRAKNO(ii)
			End if

			Select Case FARK
			Case "0"
				HAFTA1= HAFTA1 + mpsmiktari
			Case "1"
				HAFTA2= HAFTA2 + mpsmiktari
			Case "2"
				HAFTA3= HAFTA3 + mpsmiktari
			Case "3"
				HAFTA4= HAFTA4 + mpsmiktari
			Case "4"
				HAFTA5= HAFTA5 + mpsmiktari
			Case "5"
				HAFTA6= HAFTA6 + mpsmiktari
			End Select

			Sh1.Cells(satir,1).Value = tezgahgk1
			Sh1.Cells(satir,2).Value = tezgahadi
			Sh1.Cells(satir,3).Value = STOKKODU
			Sh1.Cells(satir,4).Value = STOKADI
			Sh1.Cells(satir,5).Value = HAFTA1
			Sh1.Cells(satir,6).Value = HAFTA2
			Sh1.Cells(satir,7).Value = HAFTA3
			Sh1.Cells(satir,8).Value = HAFTA4
			Sh1.Cells(satir,9).Value = HAFTA5
			Sh1.Cells(satir,10).Value = HAFTA6
			Sh1.Cells(satir,11).Value = mpsno

			satir = satir + 1
		next
	End if
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Makro Kodu : KALIP

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.KOD(i)
			kalan = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SRVP00C","KALAN_MIKTAR",LISTET.KOD(i),"")
			if kalan <> SRVPC0.PLANLANAN_OMUR then
				fark = SRVPC0.PLANLANAN_OMUR - kalan
				if fark > 0 then

					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SRVP21","")
					Set SRV = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP21T")
					nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
					SRV.AddRow

					satirNo = SRV.GetRecCount()
					SRV.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

					SRV.KOD(satirNo) = LISTET.KOD(i)
					SRV.GIREN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = fark
					Doc1.Save_Voucher()
				End if
			End if
	Next
End Sub

Hangi Tablo için : STOK66

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK66T.GetRowCount()
		STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) = Doc.Round(Agirlik_Calc(STOK66T.KOD(i), STOK66T.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 0)
		MakaraAgirlik = GetMakaraAgirligi(i)
		STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i) = STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(i) + MakaraAgirlik
		ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
	Next
	STOK66E.NOTES_1 = "Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg"
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Toplam Brut Aðýrlýk = " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg", "", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
End Sub
Function Agirlik_Calc(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Agirlik = 0
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	for j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), "")
		if uCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
			Agirlik = Agirlik + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j)
		End if
	Next
	Agirlik_Calc = Agirlik
End Function
Function GetMakaraAgirligi(satirNo)
	MakaraAgirlik = 0
	sls = InStr(1, STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo), "/")
	if sls = 0 Then Exit Function
	Makara = Left(STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo), sls-1)
	GetMakaraAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "AGIRLIK", Doc.LeftJustify(Makara, 24), "")
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	dim sayi
	sayi=0
	k=doc.d7msgbox("Listelediðiniz Kartlar Yeniden Kaydedilecek.Emin misiniz?","DÝKKAT",MB_YESNO)
	if k=6 then
		for i=1 to LISTET.GetReccount()
			'Doc.StatusbarMessage "Kaydediliyor : " & i & " / " & LISTET.GetRecCount()
			evrakno=LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
			nret=doc.load_voucher(evrakno)
			nret=doc.Save_Voucher
			sayi=sayi+1
		next
		Msgbox ("Yeniden Kayýt Ýþlemi tamamlandý.Toplam "& sayi &" adet Fatura Güncellendi.")
	else
		exit sub
	end if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CEVA
Hangi Tablo için : TNT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TNT yükleme formunu sipariþine aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(TNT00E.TESLIM) = "" or trim(TNT00E.CH_KODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve teslim yerini girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	For i = 1 to TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "TNT Yükleme Formu Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & TNT00T.GetRecCount()
'		If TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i) > 0 Then
		If TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			ST40.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = TNT00T.KOD(i)
'			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = TNT00E.TESLIM
		End if
	Next

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("TNT00E", TNT00E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO",0)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
	EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
	ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	ST40E.GK_1= "08"

	ST40T.Empty

	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
'		if i = 100 then exit sub
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Satýþ sipariþine aktarýlýyor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	Msgbox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý"
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
	Dim T_TNT00E    
	Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERIADI", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SEVK_ADRESI","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "NETAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "BRUTAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "HACIM","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SIPARIS_NO","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "NETAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "BRUTAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "HACIM","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REV_RTESTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "DURUM","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "REZ_MIKTAR","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "REZ_MIKTAR_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTable.AMBCODE = "1SEVK"

Sub RezervasyonlarveCekiListeleri()
	Call DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Call CekiListeleriniGetir()
End Sub

Sub CekiListeleriniGetir()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT1.Empty()

	QStr = "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='CL'"
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("dde34rs55", "STOK01", QStr, "", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT1.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT1.EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT1.MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK66E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i), "")
		MTableT1.MUSTERI_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT1.MUSTERI, "")
		Set S66T = Doc.Select1G("wef44dfwe42s", "STOK66T", "EVRAKNO='" & S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", "OALLOC_SIPNO", -1)
		SipNo = ""
		For j = 1 to S66T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(j)) <> "" Then
				SipNo = SipNo & ", " & trim(S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(j))
			End if
		Next
		if trim(SipNo) <> "" Then
			MTableT1.SIPARIS_NO = Mid(SipNo, 3)
		End if
		S66T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
		MTableT1.BRUTAGIRLIK = S66T.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTableT1.NETAGIRLIK = S66T.NETAGIRLIK
		MTableT1.HACIM = S66T.HACIM
	Next
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Sub DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	QStr = "SELECT STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU,STOK01.* FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.KOD=STOK01.KOD "
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK01.AMBCODE= '"& MTable.AMBCODE &"' "

	if trim(MTable.SIPNO) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK40T.EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SIPNO & "' AND STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI<>'K' "
	End if

	if trim(MTable.MUSTERIKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU='" & MTable.MUSTERIKODU & "' AND STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI<>'K' "
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY STOK01.KOD"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dde34r55", QStr)

	MTableT2.Empty()

	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.KOD, "")
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

msgbox MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO &"  -  "& MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE &"  -  "& MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS &"  -  "& MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE 

		MTableT2.MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.MUSTERI_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT2.MUSTERI, "")
		MTableT2.REV_RTESTARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "REV_RTESTARIH", S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.CHSIPNO = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK = Doc.Round(GetNetAgirlik(MTableT2.KOD(i), MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 1)

	Next
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Brüt Aðýrlýk ve Hacim Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		Call GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, i, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(i)
		MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK + AmbalajAgirligi
		MTableT2.HACIM = Hacim
	Next

	MTableT2.Sort("MUSTERI_AD")
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = 0
End Sub

Sub YeniCekiListesiYarat()
	Call CekiListesineEkle("")
End Sub

Sub SecilenCekiListesineEkle()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.Select2("wer23rrtddd", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Eklenecek çeki listesi seçmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca 1 adet çeki listesi seçebilirsiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Call CekiListesineEkle(MTableT1.EVRAKNO(1))
End Sub

Sub CekiListesineEkle(CekiListesiNo)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.Select2("wer23rrtd", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MTableT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK66", "")
	Set S66E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK66E")
	Set S66T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK66T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")

	if trim(CekiListesiNo) = "" Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		S66E.MUSTERIKODU = MTableT2.MUSTERI
		S66E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	Else
		Doc1.Load_Voucher CekiListesiNo
	End if

	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT2.SELECTED(i) = "E" and Trim(MTableT2.DURUM(i)) <> "Aktarýldý" Then
			satirNo = S66T.AddRow()
			S66T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S66T.KOD = MTableT2.KOD(i)
			S66T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S66T.SERINO = MTableT2.SERINO(i)
			S66T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S66T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_UNIT(i)
			S66T.NETAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK(i)
			S66T.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
			S66T.HACIM = MTableT2.HACIM(i)
			S66T.LOCATION1 = MTableT2.LOCATION1(i)
			S66T.LOCATION2 = MTableT2.LOCATION2(i)
			S66T.LOCATION3 = MTableT2.LOCATION3(i)
			S66T.LOCATION4 = MTableT2.LOCATION4(i)
			S66T.FOKOD = MTableT2.FOKOD(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT2.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT2.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT2.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S66T.SIPNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S66T.SIPARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT2.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

msgbox MTableT2.OALLOC_EVRAKNO &"  -  "& MTableT2.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE &"  -  "& MTableT2.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS &"  -  "& MTableT2.OALLOC_SIPTYPE 

		End if
	Next
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		Call EkraniTazele()
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT + MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM + MTableT2.HACIM
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT - MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM - MTableT2.HACIM
	End if
End Sub
Sub REZ_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("ee4r56", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MT2.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR
End Sub
Function GetNetAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar)
	GetNetAgirlik = 0
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("we3fsddsf", "MAKROTABLET2", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&NETAGIRLIK>0", "", 1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Div(MT2.NETAGIRLIK * Miktar, MT2.SF_MIKTAR)
	Else
'		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG", StokKodu, Miktar)
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Agirlik_Calc", StokKodu, Miktar)
	End if
End Function
Sub GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, satirNo, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
	AmbalajAgirligi = 0
	Hacim = 0
	if trim(MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)) <> "" Then
		AmbalajAgirligi = 15
		Hacim = 0.80 * 1.20 * 1.1   'palet x boy x yukseklik
		Set MT2 = Doc.Select2G("sdf234sfsd", "MAKROTABLET2", "VRI_VR_R1='" & MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) & "'", "", "VRI_VR_R1", -1)
		Hacim = Doc.Div(Hacim * MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), MT2.SF_MIKTAR)
	Elseif trim(MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2(satirNo)) <> "" Then
		Set A00 = Doc.Select1("Asd2q3dsaf", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='" & MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
		if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			AmbalajAgirligi = A00.AGIRLIK
			Hacim = A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_DISGENISLIK / 1000000000
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ambalaj Tanýmlarý 'nda " & trim(trim(MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2(satirNo))) & " kodlu makaraya ait ambalaj tanýmý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub EkraniTazele()
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED<>'E'", "", -1)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Alanlar = MTableT2.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = MT2.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CEKI
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çeki Listesini Ýrsaliyeye Dönüþtür
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	CekiListesiNo = InputBox("Çeki Listesi Numarasýný Giriniz")
	if trim(CekiListesiNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set S66E = Doc.Select1("sedf234gwdg", "STOK66E", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(CekiListesiNo),10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S66E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set S66T = Doc.Select1("sedf2ddd3r34gwdg", "STOK66T", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(CekiListesiNo),10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S66T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	STOK60T.SafePack
	
	STOK60E.NOTES_1 = S66E.NOTES_1
	STOK60E.NOTES_2 = S66E.NOTES_2
	STOK60E.NOTES_3 = S66E.NOTES_3
	STOK60E.NOTES_4 = trim(S66E.EVRAKNO) & " Evrak Numaralý Çeki Listesi Kullanýlmýþtýr."
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK66","")
	Set S66E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK66E")
	nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(Doc.RightJustify(trim(CekiListesiNo),10))
	S66E.NOTES_4 = trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO)
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	nret = Doc1.Loadempty_voucher()
	
	STOK60E.BOX1_1 = S66E.BOX1_1
	STOK60E.BOX1_2 = S66E.BOX1_2
	STOK60E.BOX1_3 = S66E.BOX1_3
	
	STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1 = S66E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
	STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2 = S66E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2
	
	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1 = S66E.SEVKADRESI_1
	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2 = S66E.SEVKADRESI_2
	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3 = S66E.SEVKADRESI_3
	
	chkodu = S66E.MUSTERIKODU
	adres = S66E.SHIPTOCODE
	
	For i = 1 to S66T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow()
		STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK60T.KOD(satirNo) = S66T.KOD(i)
		STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S66T.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		QStr = "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='CL' "

		STOK60T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1)
		STOK60T.FOKOD(satirNo) = S01.FOKOD(1)

		STOK60T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R1(1)
		STOK60T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R2(1)
		STOK60T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R3(1)
		STOK60T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM1(1)
		STOK60T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM2(1)
		STOK60T.LOCATION1(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION1(1)
		STOK60T.LOCATION2(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION2(1)
		STOK60T.LOCATION3(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION3(1)
		STOK60T.LOCATION4(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION4(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(1)
		STOK60T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(1)

'msgbox STOK60T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) &"  -  "& STOK60T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) &"  -  "& STOK60T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) &"  -  "& STOK60T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) 


		STOK60T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(satirNo) = S66T.REF_TEXT_1(i)
	Next
	
	'stok kartlarý ektanýmlar2 den kasa veya koli kod ve adetleri geldi
	for z = 1  to S66T.GetRowCount()
		Set S0A = Doc.Select1("s1dasd1", "STOK0A","EVRAKNO='" & S66T.KOD(z) & "' & CH_KODU='" & trim(chkodu) & "'& SHIPTOCODE='" & trim(adres) & "'", "", -1)
		if S0A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then msgbox "Seçilen kodlarýn bu cari için bir ambalaj tanýmý bulunmamaktadýr!..."
		if S0A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		for k = 1 to S0A.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S0A.AMBALAJTURU(k)) = "KASA" or trim(S0A.AMBALAJTURU(k)) = "KOLI" then
				satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow()
				STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				STOK60T.KOD(satirNo) = S0A.AMBALAJKODU(k)
				kasaadeti = Doc.Div(S66T.SF_MIKTAR(z),S0A.SF_PAKETICERIGI(k))
				STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = kasaadeti
			End if
		Next
	Next
	
	'çekli listesinden palet kod ve adetleri geldi
	Set S66T2 = Doc.Select1("frf24", "STOK66T", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(CekiListesiNo),10) & "' & AMBALAJKODU1 <> ' '", "", -1)
	if S66T2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	for x = 1 to S66T2.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S66T2.AMBALAJKODU1(x)) <> "" then
			satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow()
			STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			STOK60T.KOD(satirNo) = S66T2.AMBALAJKODU1(x)
			STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S66T2.SF_VRI_NUM1(x)
		End if
	Next
	
	'çekli listesinden palet kapaklarý kod ve adetleri geldi
	Set S66T3 = Doc.Select1("frf524", "STOK66T", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(CekiListesiNo),10) & "'& AMBALAJKODU2 <> ' '","", -1)
	if S66T3.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	for xx = 1 to S66T3.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S66T3.AMBALAJKODU2(xx)) <> "" then
			satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow()
			STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			STOK60T.KOD(satirNo) = S66T3.AMBALAJKODU2(xx)
			STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S66T3.SF_VRI_NUM1(xx)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set WB = Excel.Workbooks.Open("d:\muhaktar.xlsx")
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets("Sayfa1")
	rowBaslangic = 1
	rowBitis = 839								'	rowBitis = 250
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("CARI20","")
	Set CARIE = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI20E")
	Set CARIT = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI20T")
	Set CARIMT = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHDST")
	
	
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")
	Set MAHSUPE = Doc2.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
	Set MAHSUPT = Doc2.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
	
	Set Doc3 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("NAKT11","")
	Set KASAE = Doc3.GetTableObject("NAKT11E")
	Set KASAT = Doc3.GetTableObject("NAKT10T")
	
	satirSayisi = rowBitis - rowBaslangic
	yevmiyeno = "0"
	
	Dim bos1(50)
	Dim bos1at(50)
	Dim bos1bt(50)
	Dim bos1ac(50)
	Dim bos1kur(50)
	
	
	cr = ""
	cra = ""
	crb = ""
	crac = ""
	
	crk = 1
	crc = "TL"
	
	
	
	
	crr = ""
	crra = ""
	crrb = ""
	crrk = 1
	crrac = ""
	
	
	fisd = ""
	
	'0 boþ 1 cari 2 muhasebe
	drm = 0
	
	For kk = 1 to 50
		bos1(kk) = ""
		bos1at(kk) = ""
		bos1bt(kk) = ""
		bos1ac(kk) = ""
		bos1kur(kk) = 1
	Next
	
	
	For i = rowBaslangic to rowBitis
		Doc.StatusbarMessage (i - rowBaslangic + 1) & " / " & satirSayisi
		
		
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "Tahsil" or trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "Tediye" or trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "Mahsup" then		'		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,10).Value) = "Yevmiye No:" then
if i < 10 then msgbox i & "   -    " &  trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)

			if trim(yevmiyeno) <> trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value) then				'			if trim(yevmiyeno) <> trim(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value) then
				
				if trim(yevmiyeno) > "1" then
					if fisd = "cari" then
						if trim(bos1(1)) <> "" then
							if trim(cr) <> "" then
								CARIT.AddRow
								satirNox = CARIT.GetRecCount()
								CARIT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
								CARIT.KOD(satirNox) = trim(cr)
								if trim(cra) <> "" then
									CARIT.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cra
									CARIT.CH_KUR = crc
									CARIT.CH_KURV = crk
								else
									if trim(crb) <> "" then
										CARIT.CH_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = crb
										CARIT.CH_KUR = crc
										CARIT.CH_KURV = crk
									end if
								end if
								
								
								for kk = 1 to 50
									if trim(bos1(kk)) <> "" then
										CARIMT.AddRow
										satirNoxx = CARIMT.GetRecCount()
										CARIMT.SetCurrentRow(satirNoxx)
										CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNoxx) = bos1(kk)
										CARIMT.ACIKLAMA(satirNoxx) = bos1ac(kk)
										if trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "" then
											CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = cdbl(bos1at(kk))*bos1kur(kk)
										else
											if trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "" then
												CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = cdbl(bos1bt(kk))*bos1kur(kk)
											end if
										end if
										
										if (trim(cra) <> "") and (trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "") or (trim(crb) <> "") and (trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "") then
										else
											CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE_BA(satirNoxx) = "-"
										end if
									end if
								next
								
								
								
								
							else
								if trim(crr) <> "" then
									
									for kk = 1 to 50
										if trim(bos1(kk)) <> "" then
											CARIMT.AddRow
											satirNoxx = CARIMT.GetRecCount()
											CARIMT.SetCurrentRow(satirNoxx)
											CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNoxx) = bos1(kk)
											CARIMT.ACIKLAMA(satirNoxx) = bos1ac(kk)
											if trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "" then
												CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = cdbl(bos1at(kk))*bos1kur(kk)
											else
												if trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "" then
													CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = cdbl(bos1bt(kk))*bos1kur(kk)
												end if
											end if
											
											if (trim(crra) <> "") and (trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "") or (trim(crrb) <> "") and (trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "") then
											else
												CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE_BA(satirNoxx) = "-"
											end if
										end if
									next
									
									
									
								end if
							end if
							
							
							For kk = 1 to 50
								bos1(kk) = ""
								bos1at(kk) = ""
								bos1bt(kk) = ""
								bos1ac(kk) = ""
								bos1kur(kk) = 1
							Next
							cr = ""
							cra = ""
							crb = ""
							
							crk = 1
							crc = "TL"
							
							
							crac = ""
							
							crr = ""
							crra = ""
							crrb = ""
							crrk = 1
							crrac = ""
						end if
					end if
					
					
					
					if trim(fisd) = "nakit" then
msgbox "nakit"
						xc = Doc3.Save_Voucher()
					else
						if trim(fisd) = "cari" then
msgbox "cari"
							xc = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
						else
							if trim(fisd) = "mahsup" then
msgbox "mahsup"
								xc = Doc2.Save_Voucher()
							end if
						end if
					end if
					
					cr = ""
					cra = ""
					crb = ""
					
					
					crc = "TL"
					crk = 1
					
					crac = ""
					
					
					crr = ""
					crra = ""
					crrb = ""
					crrk = 1
					crrac = ""
					
					'0 boþ 1 cari 2 muhasebe
					drm = 0
					
					For kk = 1 to 50
						bos1(kk) = ""
						bos1at(kk) = ""
						bos1bt(kk) = ""
						bos1ac(kk) = ""
						bos1kur(kk) = ""
					Next
					
					
				end if
				
				yevmiyeno = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)				'				yevmiyeno = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)
				
				fist = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value))			'				fist = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value))
				fisacklmx = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,9).Value)				'				fisacklmx = trim(Sh1.Cells(i+1,12).Value)
				
				msgbox yevmiyeno &  "   -   -  "&yevmiyeno 
				fistarih = fist
				
				xsatir = i + 3
				
				if yevmiyeno > "1" then
					buldu = 0
					
					
					fisd = ""
					kasa = ""
					for k = xsatir to xsatir + 50
						if buldu = 0 then
							if (trim(Sh1.Cells(k,1).Value) = "") and (trim(Sh1.Cells(k,2).Value) = "") then
								buldu = 1
							else
msgbox yevmiyeno & "  bulllduu"
								d1 = ""
								d2 = ""
								d1 = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,7).Value)),1,3)				'								d1 = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,1).Value)),1,3)
								d2 = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,7).Value)),1,3)				'								d2 = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,2).Value)),1,3)
								
								if  d1 = "100" then
									fisd = "nakit"
'									kasa = "100-001"
									kasa = "KASA-TL"
									'kasa = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,1).Value)),1,3)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,1).Value)),4,2)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,1).Value)),6,3)
								end if
								if d2 = "100" then
									fisd = "nakit"
'									kasa = "100-001"
									kasa = "KASA-TL"
									'kasa = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,2).Value)),1,3)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,2).Value)),4,2)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(k,2).Value)),6,3)
								end if
								
								if (fisd <> "nakit") and ((d1 = "102") or (d1 = "120") or (d1 = "320") or (d2 = "102") or (d2 = "120") or (d2 = "320")) then
									fisd = "cari"
								end if
							end if
						end if
					next
					
					if trim(fisd) = "" then
						fisd = "mahsup"
					end if
					
					
					if fisd = "nakit" then
						k = Doc3.Load_Voucher("B"&trim(yevmiyeno))
						if k <> 0 then
							y = doc.d7MsgBox(yevmiyeno & " no.lu Evrak yüklenemedi" & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir",MB_OK)
							Doc3.LoadEmpty_Voucher
							Set Doc3 = Nothing
							Excel.Quit
							Set Excel = Nothing
							exit Sub
						else
							KASAE.TARIH = fistarih
							KASAE.KASACODE = kasa
						end if
					else
						if fisd = "cari" then
							
							k = Doc1.Load_Voucher("B"&trim(yevmiyeno))
							if k <> 0 then
								y = doc.d7MsgBox(yevmiyeno & " no.lu Evrak yüklenemedi" & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir",MB_OK)
								Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
								Set Doc1 = Nothing
								Excel.Quit
								Set Excel = Nothing
								exit Sub
							else
								CARIE.TARIH = fistarih
							end if
							
						else
							if fisd = "mahsup" then
								k = Doc2.Load_Voucher("B"&trim(yevmiyeno))
								if k <> 0 then
									y = doc.d7MsgBox(yevmiyeno & " no.lu Evrak yüklenemedi" & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir",MB_OK)
									Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
									Set Doc2 = Nothing
									Excel.Quit
									Set Excel = Nothing
									exit Sub
								else
									MAHSUPE.TARIH = fistarih
									MAHSUPE.ACIKLAMA = fisacklmx
								end if
							end if
						end if
					end if
					
				end if
			end if
			
		else
			if yevmiyeno > "1" then
				
				f1  = ""
				f2  = ""
				f1x = ""
				f2x = ""
				
				fb = ""
				fa = ""
				
				fk = 1
				fc = "TL"
				
				
				facik = ""
				f1k = ""
				f2k = ""
				f1 = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value))
				f2 = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value))
				f1k = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)),1,3)
				f2k = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)),1,3)
				
				
				'f1x = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)),1,3)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)),5,2)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)),8,3)
				'f2x = mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)),1,3)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)),5,2)+" "+mid(trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)),8,3)
				
				
				f1x = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value))
				f2x = trim(cstr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value))
				
				
				facik = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value
				
				if (trim(f1) <> "Fiþ Tarihi :") and (trim(f1) <> "Açýklama :") and (trim(f1) <> "Hesap Kodu") and (trim(f1) <> "Düzenleyen :") and (trim(f1) <> "Kontrol Eden :") then
					if (trim(f1) <> "") or (trim(f2) <> "") then
						
						
						
						
						
						
						if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value) <> "" then
							fb = Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value
							fk = cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,8).Value)/cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)
							if fk < 2 then
								fc = "USD"
							else
								fc = "EUR"
							end if
						else
							fb = Sh1.Cells(i,8).Value
							if trim(fb) <> "" then
								fk = 1
								fc = "TL"
							end if
						end if
						
						if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value) <> "" then
							fa = Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value
							fk = cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,9).Value)/cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)
							if fk < 2 then
								fc = "USD"
							else
								fc = "EUR"
							end if
						else
							fa = Sh1.Cells(i,9).Value
							if trim(fa) <> "" then
								fk = 1
								fc = "TL"
							end if
						end if
						
						
						if trim(fisd) = "nakit" then
							
							if (f1k = "102") or (f1k = "120") or (f1k = "320") then
								KASAT.AddRow
								satirNox = KASAT.GetRecCount()
								KASAT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
								KASAT.CARICODE(satirNox) = trim(f1x)
								if trim(fa) <> "" then
									KASAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fa)
								end if
								if trim(fb) <> "" then
									KASAT.OR_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fb)
								end if
								if trim(facik) <> "" then
									KASAT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = facik
								end if
								KASAT.OR_KUR(satirNox) = fc
								KASAT.KURV(satirNox) = fk
							else
								if (f1k <> "100") and (trim(f1k) <> "") then
									KASAT.AddRow
									satirNox = KASAT.GetRecCount()
									KASAT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
									KASAT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNox) = trim(f1x)
									if trim(fa) <> "" then
										KASAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fa)
									end if
									if trim(fb) <> "" then
										KASAT.OR_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fb)
									end if
									if trim(facik) <> "" then
										KASAT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = trim(facik)
									end if
									KASAT.OR_KUR(satirNox) = fc
									KASAT.KURV(satirNox) = fk
								end if
							end if
							
							
							if (f2k = "102") or (f2k = "120") or (f2k = "320") then
								KASAT.AddRow
								satirNox = KASAT.GetRecCount()
								KASAT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
								KASAT.CARICODE(satirNox) = trim(f2x)
								if trim(fa) <> "" then
									KASAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fa)
								end if
								if trim(fb) <> "" then
									KASAT.OR_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fb)
								end if
								if trim(facik) <> "" then
									KASAT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = trim(facik)
								end if
								KASAT.OR_KUR(satirNox) = fc
								KASAT.KURV(satirNox) = fk
								
							else
								if (trim(f2k) <> "100") and (trim(f2k) <> "") then
									KASAT.AddRow
									satirNox = KASAT.GetRecCount()
									KASAT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
									KASAT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNox) = trim(f2x)
									if trim(fa) <> "" then
										KASAT.OR_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fa)
									end if
									if trim(fb) <> "" then
										KASAT.OR_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = trim(fb)
									end if
									if trim(facik) <> "" then
										KASAT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = trim(facik)
									end if
									KASAT.OR_KUR(satirNox) = fc
									KASAT.KURV(satirNox) = fk
								end if
							end if
							
							
						else
							if trim(fisd) = "cari" then
								'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
								'cr = "",cra = "",crb = "", drm 0 boþ 1 cari 2 muhasebe,bos1(kk) = "",bos1at(kk) = "",bos1bt(kk) = ""
								
								if (f1k = "120") or (f1k = "320") or (f1k = "102") or (f2k = "120") or (f2k = "320") or (f2k = "102") then
									
									if cr = "" then
										crac = facik
										if (f1k = "120") or (f1k = "320") or (f1k = "102") then
											cr = f1x
										else
											if (f2k = "120") or (f2k = "320") or (f2k = "102") then
												cr = f2x
											end if
										end if
										
										
										if trim(fb) <> "" then
											crb = trim(fb)
											crk = fk
											crc = fc
										else
											if trim(fa) <> "" then
												cra = trim(fa)
												crk = fk
												crc = fc
											end if
										end if
										
										if trim(bos1(1)) <> "" then
											'carimuhasebeekle
											
											'//////////////////////////////////////////////////////
											
											CARIT.AddRow
											satirNox = CARIT.GetRecCount()
											CARIT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
											CARIT.KOD(satirNox) = cr
											
											if trim(cra) <> "" then
												CARIT.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cra
											else
												if trim(crb) <> "" then
													CARIT.CH_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = crb
												end if
											end if
											
											CARIT.CH_KUR(satirNox) = crc
											CARIT.CH_KURV(satirNox) = crk
											
											
											
											'msgbox(trim(cr)&" / "&trim(cra)&" / "&trim(crb))
											
											for kk = 1 to 50
												if trim(bos1(kk)) <> "" then
													CARIMT.AddRow
													satirNoxx = CARIMT.GetRecCount()
													CARIMT.SetCurrentRow(satirNoxx)
													CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNoxx) = trim(bos1(kk))
													CARIMT.ACIKLAMA(satirNoxx) = trim(bos1ac(kk))
													if trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "" then
														CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = bos1at(kk)*bos1kur(kk)
													else
														if trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "" then
															CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = bos1bt(kk)*bos1kur(kk)
														end if
													end if
													
													
													'msgbox(trim(bos1(kk))&" / "&trim(bos1at(kk))&" / "&trim(bos1bt(kk)))
													
													
													if (trim(cra) <> "") and (trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "") or (trim(crb) <> "") and (trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "") then
													else
														CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE_BA(satirNoxx) = "-"
													end if
												end if
											next
											
											
											'//////////////////////////////////////////////////////
											
											
											For kk = 1 to 50
												bos1(kk) = ""
												bos1at(kk) = ""
												bos1bt(kk) = ""
												bos1ac(kk) = ""
												bos1kur(kk) = 1
											Next
											
											crr = trim(cr)
											crra = trim(cra)
											crrb = trim(crb)
											crrac = trim(crac)
											crrk = crk
											
											cr = ""
											cra = ""
											crb = ""
											crac = ""
											
											crk = 1
											crc = "TL"
										end if
									else
										if bos1(1) = "" then
											'caricariekle
											
											'///////////////////////////////////
											CARIT.AddRow
											satirNox = CARIT.GetRecCount()
											CARIT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
											CARIT.KOD(satirNox) = cr
											if trim(crac) <> "" then
												CARIT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = crac
											end if
											
											if trim(f1x) <> "" then
												CARIT.KARSI_CODE(satirNox) = trim(f1x)
											else
												if trim(f2x) <> "" then
													CARIT.KARSI_CODE(satirNox) = trim(f2x)
												end if
											end if
											
											
											if trim(cra) <> "" then
												CARIT.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cra
											else
												if trim(crb) <> "" then
													CARIT.CH_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = crb
												end if
											end if
											
											CARIT.CH_KUR(satirNox) = crc
											CARIT.CH_KURV(satirNox) = crk
											
											
											
											'///////////////////////////////////
											
											cr = ""
											cra = ""
											crb = ""
											crac = ""
											
											crk = 1
											crc = "TL"
											
											
											crr = ""
											crra = ""
											crrb = ""
											crrac = ""
											crrk = 1
										else
											'oncekicarimuhasebeekle
											'////////////////////////////////////////////
											CARIT.AddRow
											satirNox = CARIT.GetRecCount()
											CARIT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
											CARIT.KOD(satirNox) = cr
											if trim(cra) <> "" then
												CARIT.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cra
											else
												if trim(crb) <> "" then
													CARIT.CH_BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = crb
												end if
											end if
											
											
											CARIT.CH_KUR(satirNox) = crc
											CARIT.CH_KURV(satirNox) = crk
											
											
											for kk = 1 to 50
												if trim(bos1(kk)) <> "" then
													CARIMT.AddRow
													satirNoxx = CARIMT.GetRecCount()
													CARIMT.SetCurrentRow(satirNoxx)
													CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE(satirNoxx) = bos1(kk)
													if trim(bos1ac(kk)) <> "" then
														CARIMT.ACIKLAMA(satirNoxx) = bos1ac(kk)
													end if
													
													if trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "" then
														CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = bos1at(kk)*bos1kur(kk)
													else
														if trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "" then
															CARIMT.MUHASEBEFIYAT(satirNoxx) = bos1bt(kk)*bos1kur(kk)
														end if
													end if
													
													if (trim(cra) <> "") and (trim(bos1bt(kk)) <> "") or (trim(crb) <> "") and (trim(bos1at(kk)) <> "") then
													else
														CARIMT.MUHASEBECODE_BA(satirNoxx) = "-"
													end if
												end if
											next
											
											'////////////////////////////////////////////
											For kk = 1 to 50
												bos1(kk) = ""
												bos1at(kk) = ""
												bos1bt(kk) = ""
												bos1ac(kk) = ""
												bos1kur(kk) = 1
											Next
											
											crr = ""
											crra = ""
											crrb = ""
											crrac = ""
											crrk = 1
											
											crac = facik
											if (f1 = "120") or (f1 = "320") or (f1 = "102") then
												cr = f1x
											else
												if (f2 = "120") or (f2 = "320") or (f2 = "102") then
													cr = f2x
												end if
											end if
											if trim(fb) <> "" then
												crb = fb
											else
												if trim(fa) <> "" then
													cra = fa
												end if
											end if
										end if
									end if
								else
									'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
									varmi = 0
									For kk = 1 to 50
										if varmi = 0 then
											if bos1(kk) = "" then
												if trim(f1x) <> "" then
													bos1(kk) = trim(f1x)
												else
													if trim(f2x) <> "" then
														bos1(kk) = trim(f2x)
													end if
												end if
												
												bos1ac(kk) = facik
												if trim(fb) <> "" then
													bos1bt(kk) = cdbl(fb)
												end if
												if trim(fa) <> "" then
													bos1at(kk) = cdbl(fa)
												end if
												
												bos1kur(kk) = cdbl(fk)
												
												varmi = 1
											end if
										end if
									Next
									'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
								end if
								'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
							else
								'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
								if (trim(f1x) <> "") or (trim(f2x) <> "") then
									MAHSUPT.AddRow
									satirNox = MAHSUPT.GetRecCount()
									MAHSUPT.SetCurrentRow(satirNox)
									if trim(f1x) <> "" then
										MAHSUPT.KOD(satirNox) = trim(f1x)
									else
										MAHSUPT.KOD(satirNox) = trim(f2x)
									end if
									
									
									if fk = 1 then
										if trim(fa) <> "" then
											MAHSUPT.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fa)
										else
											if trim(fb) <> "" then
												MAHSUPT.BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fb)
											end if
										end if
									else
										
										
										
										if trim(fa) <> "" then
											MAHSUPT.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fa)*cdbl(fk)
											MAHSUPT.ALTALACAKFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fa)
											MAHSUPT.ALTPB(satirNox) = trim(fc)
										else
											if trim(fb) <> "" then
												MAHSUPT.BORCFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fb)*cdbl(fk)
												MAHSUPT.ALTBORCFIYAT(satirNox) = cdbl(fb)
												MAHSUPT.ALTPB(satirNox) = trim(fc)
											end if
										end if
										
										
									end if
									
									if trim(facik) <> "" then
										MAHSUPT.ACIKLAMA(satirNox) = trim(facik)
									end if
									
									
								end if
								'////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
							end if
						end if
						
					end if
				end if
			end if
		end if
		
	Next
	
	if trim(fisd) = "nakit" then
msgbox "nakit111"
		xc = Doc3.Save_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(fisd) = "nakit" then
msgbox "nakit222"
			xc = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		else
msgbox "nakit333"
			xc = Doc2.Save_Voucher()
		end if
	end if
	
	
	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc3.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	
	Set Doc1 = Nothing
	Set Doc2 = Nothing
	Set Doc3 = Nothing
	
	Excel.Quit
	Set Excel = Nothing
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TOFAS
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Tofaþ GÜNLÜK Programýný satýþ sipariþine aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
'	Set S40E = GetKapanacakSipList()
'Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.EXCELOUT", "SendTableToExcel", S40E
'msgbox S40E.GetRowCount()
'exit sub

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40G = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")

	For i = 1 to OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþler okunuyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		ProgramNo = OTOSP1L.PROGRAM_NO(i)
		Attrib = OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i)
		TesTarih = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
		Miktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		FabrikaKodu = OTOSP1L.FABRIKA_KODU(i)
		RampaKodu = OTOSP1L.ARA_SEVK(i)

		if (Attrib = "G" or Attrib = "H") and Miktar > 0 Then
			StokKodu = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
			if trim(ProgramNo) = "KESIN" Then
				if OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
					satirNo = ST40G.AddRow
					ST40G.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
					ST40G.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
					ST40G.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
					ST40G.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
					if trim(FabrikaKodu) = "129" Then
						ST40G.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = "T" & trim(FabrikaKodu) & "-" & trim(RampaKodu)
					Else
						ST40G.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = "T" & trim(FabrikaKodu)
					End if
					ST40G.ACIKKAPALI(satirNo) = Attrib
				End if
			Else
				satirNo = ST40.AddRow
				ST40.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
				ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
				ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
				ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
				ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = FabrikaKodu
				ST40.ACIKKAPALI(satirNo) = Attrib
			End if
			OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
		end if
	Next
	OTOSP1L.SafePack
	ST40.Sort("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	
	for i = 1 to ST40G.GetRecCount()
		if ST40G.RTESTARIH(i) < Doc.BuGun Then
			ST40G.RTESTARIH(i) = Doc.BuGun
			ST40G.REV_RTESTARIH(i) = Doc.BuGun
		End if
	Next
	ST40G.GroupBy("CHSIPNO+REV_RTESTARIH+KOD")
	ST40G.Sort("CHSIPNO+REV_RTESTARIH")

'	Call ExceleYaz(ST40)
'	exit sub
	Call GunlukOngoruleriAktar(ST40)
	Call SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40G)

	OTOSP1E.ACIKKAPALI = "K"
	Doc.Save_Voucher

	doc.d7Msgbox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "Freedom", MB_OK
End Sub
Sub GunlukOngoruleriAktar(ST40)
	if ST40.GetRecCount() = 0 Then exit sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set K = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

	EvrakSayisi = 1
	EvrakSatirSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(ST40.GetRecCount(), EvrakSayisi))
	SiparisSiraNo = 0
	EvrakNo = GetEvrakNo(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		if i mod EvrakSatirSayisi = 1 and i <> 1 then
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			if nret <> 0 then
				k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
			SiparisSiraNo = SiparisSiraNo + 1
			EvrakNo = "T" & SiparisSiraNO
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
			if nret <> 0 then
				k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
			ST40T.Empty
			ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
			ST40E.TARIH = Doc.BuGun
			ST40E.GK_1 = "FOSN"
			ST40E.HOLDTOSEVK = "ONGORU"
		elseif i = 1 then
'			EvrakNo = "T" & trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(i))
'			EvrakNo = "T0"
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
			if nret <> 0 then
				k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
			ST40T.Empty
			ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
			ST40E.TARIH = Doc.BuGun
			ST40E.GK_1 = "ONGORU"
			ST40E.HOLDTOSEVK = "E"
			ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""
			ST40E.FIYATLISTESI = ""
			ST40E.CHSIPNO = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(i))
		end if
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþ oluþturuluyor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40)
	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set K = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

	Call OncekiKesinSiparisleriKapat()
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		if EskiSevk <> ST40.CHSIPNO(i) Then
			if trim(EskiSevk) <> "" Then
				nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
				if nret <> 0 then
					k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
					Exit Sub
				end if
			end if
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
			ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
			ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(i))
			ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""
			ST40E.FIYATLISTESI = ""
			ST40T.Empty
			Sipler = Sipler & trim(ST40E.EVRAKNO) & ", " 
		End if
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sevk edilecek sipariþiler yaratýlýyor : " & ST40E.EVRAKNO & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		EskiSevk = ST40.CHSIPNO(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
'	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	if Len(Sipler) > 2 Then
		Msgbox Left(Sipler, Len(Sipler) - 2) & " no.lu sipariþ(ler) yaratýldý"
	end if
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
	Dim T_TNT00E    
	Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub
Sub ExceleYaz(ST40)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set WB = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh = WB.Sheets(1)
	for i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel'e yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount
		Sh.Cells(i,1) = ST40.KOD(i)
		Sh.Cells(i,2) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Sh.Cells(i,3) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		Sh.Cells(i,4) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	Excel.Visible = True
'	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetMiktarTarih(StokKodu, Miktar, Tarih, Attrib)
	Dim MiktarArray()
	Redim MiktarArray(1,0)
	if Attrib = "G" then
		MiktarArray(0,0) = Miktar
		MiktarArray(1,0) = Tarih
		GetMiktarTarih = MiktarArray
		exit Function
	end if

	Gun = GetHaftaninGunu(Tarih)
'	PartiBuyuklugu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU", StokKodu, "")
'	if PartiBuyuklugu = 0 then PartiBuyuklugu = Miktar
'	if PartiBuyuklugu >= Miktar then
'		MiktarArray(0,0) = Miktar
'		MiktarArray(1,0) = Tarih
'		GetMiktarTarih = MiktarArray
'		exit Function
'	end if

	if Gun > 5 then Gun = 5
	KacaBolunecek = 5 - Gun + 1

	GunlukMiktar = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, KacaBolunecek))

	KalanMiktar = Miktar
	While KalanMiktar > 0
		sirano = siraNo + 1
		if KalanMiktar > GunlukMiktar then
			SipMiktari = GunlukMiktar
		Else
			SipMiktari = KalanMiktar
		end if
		KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - SipMiktari

		MiktarArray(0, uBound(MiktarArray,2)) = SipMiktari
		TeslimTarihi = GetTarihGun(Tarih, Gun + SiraNo - 1)
		MiktarArray(1, uBound(MiktarArray,2)) = TeslimTarihi
		if KalanMiktar > 0 then
			Redim Preserve MiktarArray(1, uBound(MiktarArray,2) + 1)
		end if
		if sirano > 100 then
			msgbox "Döngü 100 'den fazla devam etti. Hatayý kontrol ediniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir" 
			Exit Function
		end if
	Wend
	GetMiktarTarih = MiktarArray
End Function
Function GetHaftaninGunu(Tarih)
	dim d7Date, k, Gun
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	k = d7Date.SetDate(Tarih)
	GetHaftaninGunu = d7Date.GetDOW
End Function
Function GetTarihGun(Tarih, KacinciGun)
	HatfaninGunu = GetHaftaninGunu(Tarih)
	Fark = KacinciGun - HatfaninGunu
	GetTarihGun = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Tarih, Fark)
End Function
Sub SatirSil(satirNo)
	OTOSP1L.KOD(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0
	OTOSP1L.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.ASN_NO(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = ""
	OTOSP1L.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = 0
	OTOSP1L.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = 0

'	TableFields = OTOSP1L.GetFields()
'	for i = 0 to uBound(TableFields, 1)
'		if TableFields(i, 2) = "CHAR" or TableFields(i, 2) = "DATE" or TableFields(i, 2) = "BYTE" Then
'			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1L", TableFields(i, 0), satirNo, Space(TableFields(i, 4)))
'		Else
'			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1L", TableFields(i, 0), satirNo, 0)
'		End if
'	Next
End Sub
Function GetEvrakNo(FabrikaKodu)
	FabrikaKodu = trim(FabrikaKodu)
	if FabrikaKodu = "129" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T2"
	Elseif FabrikaKodu = "161" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T1"
	Elseif FabrikaKodu = "162" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T2"
	Elseif FabrikaKodu = "205" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T3"
	Elseif FabrikaKodu = "713" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T4"
	Elseif FabrikaKodu = "854" Then
		GetEvrakNo = "T5"
	Else
		GetEvrakNo = "T6"
	End if
End Function
Sub OncekiKesinSiparisleriKapat()
	Set S40T1 = GetKapanacakSipList()

	if S40T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set S40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	EskiSipNo = ""
	For i = 1 to S40T1.GetRowCount()
		if S40T1.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiSipNo Then
			if trim(S40E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Doc1.Load_Voucher S40T1.EVRAKNO(i)
		End if
		EskiSipNo = S40T1.EVRAKNO(i)
		satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(S40T1.SRNUM(i))
		if satirNo <= S40T.GetRowCount() Then
			S40T.ACIKKAPALI(satirNo) = "K"
		End if
	Next
	if trim(S40E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub
Function GetKapanacakSipList()
	OTOSP1L.SafePack()
	Set OSP1L = Doc.Select2G("sdfgerjhsdj", "OTOSP1L", "", "", "FABRIKA_KODU+ARA_SEVK", -1)
	InStr1 = ""
	For i = 1 to OSP1L.GetRowCount()
		SevkAdresKodu = "T" & trim(OSP1L.FABRIKA_KODU(i))
		if trim(OSP1L.ARA_SEVK(i)) <> "" Then
			SevkAdresKodu = SevkAdresKodu & "-" & trim(OSP1L.ARA_SEVK(i))
		End if
		SevkAdresKodu = Doc.RightJustify(trim(SevkAdresKodu), 10)

		InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & SevkAdresKodu & "'"
		if i < OSP1L.GetRowCount() Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
	Next

	Set OSP1L = Doc.Select2G("sdfgerjhsdj", "OTOSP1L", "", "", "KOD+REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
	InStr2 = ""
	For i = 1 to OSP1L.GetRowCount()
		InStr2 = InStr2 & "'" & OSP1L.KOD(i) & OSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i) & "'"
		if i < OSP1L.GetRowCount() Then InStr2 = InStr2 & ","
	Next

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO, SRNUM FROM STOK40T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MUSTERIKODU='" & OTOSP1E.CH_KODU & "' AND ACIKKAPALI=' ' "     '---- AND HOLDTOSEVK<>'E'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND EVRAKNO<>'" & Doc.RightJustify("T2", 10) & "' AND (SHIPTOCODE IN (" & InStr1 & ") or trim(EVRAKNO)='" & Doc.RightJustify("T0", 10) & "')"
	if Doc.IsSQL Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND KOD || REV_RTESTARIH IN (" & InStr2 & ")"
	Else
		QStr = QStr & " AND KOD + REV_RTESTARIH IN (" & InStr2 & ")"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO"
'msgbox QStr
	Set S40E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgahah", QStr)

	Set GetKapanacakSipList = S40E
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Dosya = Excel.Workbooks.Open("D:\KTA.xlsx")
	Set Sh1 = Dosya.Sheets(1)
	
	Dim i
	i=5
	bos = ""
	
	While trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).value) <> ""
		stokkodu = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).value)
		tanimlama = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,6).value)
		if trim(stokkodu) <> "" then
			Doc.Load_Voucher(trim(stokkodu))
		
			Dim T_STOK00
			Set T_STOK00=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
			Dim nret
			nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK00",T_STOK00.KOD)
			Dim T_U1
			Set T_U1=Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("KDV_IADESI",-1,trim(tanimlama))
			T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
		i = i + 1
	Wend
End Sub
