Sub Makro1()
	Dim Sql
	RefNo=MMPS10E.Evrakno
	Hazirlayan=Doc.Getd7UserName()
	Durum="Sýrada"  
	If TRIM(MMPS10E.BOMU01_FOYNO)<>"" THEN 
		ProsesNo= MMPS10E.BOMU01_FOYNO
	ELSE
		ProsesNo=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BOMU01E", "EVRAKNO", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU+"*","MAMULCODE+AKTIF_PASIF")
	END IF
	KalipGrup=ProsesNo 
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("SDE", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", 1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MamulMiktari = MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
		BaskiMiktari = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)

		rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE+AKTIF_PASIF", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "*")
		if rows > 0 Then
			Set B01E = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
			MSayi = B01E.MAMULMIKTAR(1)
		End if

		BaskiMiktari= DOC.DIV( BaskiMiktari , MSayi )
		
		Tezgah = TRIM(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(1))
		Kalip = TRIM(M10T.KALIPKODU(1))
		if trim(Kalip) = "" Then 
			Doc.D7MsgBox "Kalýp kodu girilmemiþ", "Hata", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		ToplamSure = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1) + M10T.R_MIKTAR1(1) + M10T.R_MIKTAR2(1)

		SVYUZDE = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "B_PLANCALISMAYUZDE", Tezgah, "")
		IF SVYUZDE="" or SVYUZDE=0 THEN SVYUZDE=100

		Setup = Doc.Round(M10T.R_MIKTAR1(1) * 60,1)
		Cevrim = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(1) * 3600*(SVYUZDE/100), BaskiMiktari ),0)
		
		AdamSayisi = M10T.OPERATORSAYISI1(1) + M10T.OPERATORSAYISI2(1) + M10T.OPERATORSAYISI3(1)
		JobNo= TRIM(M10T.JOBNO(1))
	End if

	tCevrim=(Cevrim * 1.15)
	tNetCevrim= Cevrim
	tTakma=Setup 
	tSokme=0

	Sql = "SELECT  * from SOPMACHINE WHERE NAME='"& Tezgah &"'"
	Set oConn = CreateObject("ADODB.CONNECTION")
	Set OraRecordSet = CreateObject("ADODB.Recordset")
	OraRecordSet.CursorLocation = 2
'********
	oConn.Open = "Provider=MSDAORA.1;Password=a;User ID=farplas2008;Data Source=DRK;Persist Security Info=True"
	OraRecordSet.Open Sql, oConn
   
	IF Not OraRecordSet.EOF then
  		PlanMakine= OraRecordSet("ID")
	ELSEIF Tezgah="BS4" THEN
		PlanMakine= 61
	ELSEIF Tezgah="B1" THEN
		PlanMakine= 60	
	End If
	OraRecordSet.CLOSE

	Sql = "SELECT  * from INJMOULD WHERE CODE='"& Kalip &"'"
	OraRecordSet.Open Sql, oConn

	IF Not OraRecordSet.EOF then
  		OracleKalipId = OraRecordSet("ID")
	End If
	OraRecordSet.CLOSE

	CevrimSayisi=MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR

	Numune=0

	IF MMPS10E.GK_1="DENEME" THEN 
		Numune=1
	END IF
	Manual=0
	Termin= MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH
	Termin= Right(Termin,2) & "." & mid(Termin,6,2) & "." & left(termin,4)

	If PlanMakine<>"" then
	     str=  "Select * from INJSETMACHINE WHERE MOULD='" & OracleKalipId & "' AND MACHINE='" & PlanMakine &"'"
			OraRecordSet.Open str, oConn

       if (OraRecordSet.EOF) then
				OraRecordSet.Close
				Cevrim = InputBox("Yeni Makine için Çevrim Süresi?", "dinamo","")
				Setup = InputBox("Yeni Makine için Setup Süresi?", "dinamo","")
				str="INSERT INTO INJSETMACHINE (MOULD, MACHINE,TLOAD) VALUES ('" & OracleKalipId & "', '" & PlanMakine  & "' , "&  Doc.CDbl(Setup) & ")" 
				OraRecordSet.Open Str, oConn

				Set OraRecSet2 = CreateObject("ADODB.Recordset")

				str="Select * FROM INJSET CROSS JOIN  INJSETMACHINE "
				str= str & " INNER  JOIN MACHINE ON INJSETMACHINE.MACHINE= SOPMACHINE.ID "
				str= str & " WHERE INJSET.MOULD=INJSETMACHINE.MOULD AND (INJSET.MOULD=" & OracleKalipId & ")"
				OraRecordSet.Open str, oConn
				OraRecordSet.Movefirst

			  While not OraRecordSet.Eof
     			str= "Select * from INJSETDURATION WHERE SETID=" & OraRecordSet("ID")  & " AND MACHINE=" & OraRecordSet("MACHINE")
     			OraRecSet2.Open str, oConn
			  		if OraRecSet2.Eof then
			     	STR="INSERT INTO INJSETDURATION (SETID, MACHINE , TPLANCYCLE, TSTDCYCLE) values (" & OraRecordSet("ID") & "," & OraRecordSet("MACHINE") & " , "&  cevrim * 1.15  &", "&  cevrim  &") "	     			
						OraRecSet2.Close
						OraRecSet2.Open str, oConn
					else
						OraRecSet2.Close
				  end if
		     OraRecordSet.MoveNext
			 	Wend

       End if
			OraRecordSet.Close
		end if
		
	If PlanMakine <>"" then
		Sql ="SELECT  * from NPPLAN WHERE RN=" + RefNo
		OraRecordSet.Open Sql, oConn
		IF Not OraRecordSet.EOF then
			sql= "UPDATE NPPLAN SET  SETID= " & KalipGrup & ", planprodtime=" & tCevrim &",netprodtime="& tNetCevrim &",dsetup1="& tTakma &" ,m="& PlanMakine&",amount="& CevrimSayisi &" ,sample="& Numune &" " 
			sql= sql & "  ,DN_MPSNO='" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "' ,DN_JOBNO='"& JobNo & "'"  
			'sql= sql & " , status="& Durum 
			sql= sql & " WHERE (RN="& RefNo &")"
		else
			sql= "INSERT INTO NPPLAN (RN, recordcreator, status, setid, planprodtime,netprodtime,dsetup1 ,m,amount ,sample,dispatchdate, DN_MPSNO, DN_JOBNO)  VALUES "
			sql= sql & "(" & RefNo & ",'" & Hazirlayan & "' ,1," & KalipGrup & "," & tCevrim & "," & tNetCevrim & "," & tTakma & "," & PlanMakine & "," & CevrimSayisi & "," & Numune & ",'" & Termin & "', '" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "', '" & JobNo & "')"

		End If
		OraRecordSet.CLOSE
		'if trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO) = "158272" Then
		'	Set de = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
		'	Doc.d7MsgBox Sql, "", MB_OK
		'End if
		OraRecordSet.Open Sql, oConn
		MsgBox "MPS Aktarýldý"
	End If

End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : AK_SP
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Alkor - Müþteri Öngörülerindem Satýþ Sipariþlerine Aktarma  (Önceki sipariþler kapanýyor) (14.05.12)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40G = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")
	Set OTOSP1_E = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	OTOSP1_E.ACIKKAPALI="K"
	Uyari = ""
	Set k = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
	For i = 1 to OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþler okunuyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		
		StokKodu = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
		TesTarih = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
		RevTesTarih = OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		Miktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Programno = OTOSP1L.PROGRAM_NO(i)
		satir_no = OTOSP1L.REF_TEXT_205(i)
		
		OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
		ST40G.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40G.GetRecCount()
		ST40G.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo) = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
		ST40G.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
		ST40G.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
		ST40G.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
		ST40G.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = RevTesTarih
		ST40G.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) =  Programno
		ST40G.REF_TEXT_205(satirNo) = satir_no

		Set S40TS = Doc.Select1g("ghjkkl", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' ' & MUSTERIKODU='"+OTOSP1E.CH_KODU+"' & HOLDTOSEVK <> 'E' & KOD = '"& OTOSP1L.KOD(i) &"' & REV_RTESTARIH = '"& RevTesTarih &"'", "","KOD+REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
		if S40TS.GetRecCount() = 0 then
			Uyari = Uyari & OTOSP1L.KOD(i) & vbcrlf
		Else
			if Miktar < S40TS.SF_MIKTAR(1) then Uyari = Uyari & OTOSP1L.KOD(i) & vbcrlf
		End if
	Next

	if trim(Uyari) <> "" Then
		Uyari = "Bir Önceki Sipariþe Göre Miktar Artýþý Gerçekleþen ürünler :" & vbcrlf & Uyari
			Doc.d7MsgBox Uyari, "", MB_OK
	End if
	Set k = Nothing

	Call SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40G)
	Call OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	
End Sub

Sub SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40)
	
	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	
	Call OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set K_b1 = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = ST40.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	ST40E.FIYSEKLI = "DF"
	ST40E.CHSIPNO = ST40.PROGRAM_NO(1)
	
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþ yaratýlýyor : " & ST40E.EVRAKNO & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()
		
		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.CHSIPANO(satirNo) = ST40.REF_TEXT_205(i)
		
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	
	if Len(Sipler) > 2 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Left(Sipler, Len(Sipler) - 2) & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
	end if
End Sub
Sub OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor..."
	
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("ghjkkl", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' ' & MUSTERIKODU='"+OTOSP1E.CH_KODU+"' & HOLDTOSEVK <> 'E'", "", -1)
	
	if S40T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	S40T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Bulunan = 0
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Bulunan = 0
		Doc1.Load_Voucher S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor : " & S40T.EVRAKNO(i) & " - " & j & " / " & ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Bulunan = Bulunan + 1

			if ST40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(j) = 0 Then
				ST40T.KOD(j) = ""
				ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(j) = ""
				ST40T.RTESTARIH(j) = ""
			Else
				ST40T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = "K"
			End if
		Next
	Next

	if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Set OSP1E = Doc.Select1("dw466y4y", "OTOSP1E", "CH_KODU='" & OTOSP1E.CH_KODU & "'&ACIKKAPALI='A'&EVRAKNO<>'" & OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if OSP1E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("OTOSP1", "")
	Set O1E = Doc1.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	For i = 1 to OSP1E.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher OSP1E.EVRAKNO(i)
		O1E.ACIKKAPALI = "K"
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Public tarih_al
Sub Makro1() 
	 
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Call Musteri_Ongoruleri(Sh1)
	Call Satis_Ongoruleri(Sh1)
	Call Kesin_Siparis(Sh1)
	CAll Onceki_Yil_Gerceklesen(Sh1)
	CAll Bu_Yil_Gerceklesen(Sh1)
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub

Sub Musteri_Ongoruleri(Sh1)

Dim AY, OTOSP_1L, append_otosp1l, j, Ap_Oto
Dim OTO_Handle, OTO_AKTAR, otosp_12332, Ap_Oto_XX21
	Doc.CreateTableObject "append_otosp1l=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set Ap_Oto = Doc.GetTableObject("append_otosp1l")

	Doc.CreateTableObject "otosp_12332=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set Ap_Oto_XX21 = Doc.GetTableObject("otosp_12332")

	Set OTOSP_1E = Doc.Select1("DASD3432OLK", "OTOSP1E", "ACIKKAPALI ='A'", "", -1) 
  tarih_al = Doc.StrDate_YEAR(OTOSP_1E.TARIH(1))

	Set OTOSP_1L = Doc.Select1("OTO_Handle", "OTOSP1L", "ACIKKAPALI ='A' ", "", -1) '// cari ve grup kodu 1 e göre
	Ap_Oto.AppendTable(OTOSP_1L)		

	'/ sýralama önceside  tabloyu kopyalýyoruz.
	Ap_Oto_XX21.CopyTable(Ap_Oto)

 	'/ Koda göre icmal alma ve evrak noya göre sýralama
	Ap_Oto.GroupBy2("KOD")
	Ap_Oto.SORT("KOD")

'Sh1.Columns("A:F").Select
'Sh1.Range("A:F").Select
	Sh1.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 5
	Sh1.Columns("B:BZ").ColumnWidth = 15

	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = "Evrak Nno"
	Sh1.Cells(1,2).Value = "Kod"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Value = "Miktar"

	MyArray = Array("Ocak", "Subat", "Mart", "Nisan", "Mayý", "Haziran", "Temmuz", "Agustos", "Eylul", "Ekim", "Kasým", "Aralýk") 

	For r = 1 To UBound(MyArray) + 1
	yer_atla = 0
	if r > 1 Then yer_atla = 5
		Sh1.Cells(1,4 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Sat.Ön. "& MyArray(r-1)
		Sh1.Cells(1,5 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Müþ.Ön. "&MyArray(r-1)
		Sh1.Cells(1,6 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Kes.Sip "&MyArray(r-1)	
		Sh1.Cells(1,7 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Geç.Fat "&MyArray(r-1)	
		Sh1.Cells(1,8 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Bu.Fat "&MyArray(r-1)
	Next

	For i = 1  To Ap_Oto.GetRecCount() 
		Sh1.Cells(i+1,1).Value = Ap_Oto.EVRAKNO(i)
		Sh1.Cells(i+1,2).Value = Ap_Oto.KOD(i)
		Sh1.Cells(i+1,3).Value = Ap_Oto.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next

Dim satir_no
	For i = 1  To Ap_Oto.GetRecCount()  '/ ÝLK LÝSTEDEKÝ KODA KARÞILIK GELEN MÝKTARLARI YERLEÞTÝRME
			For j = 1  To Ap_Oto_XX21.GetRecCount() 
				if Ap_Oto.KOD(i) = Ap_Oto_XX21.KOD(j) Then
					AY = Doc.StrDate_Month(Ap_Oto_XX21.RTESTARIH(j)) 
					yer_atla_y = 0
					For r = 1 To 12 				
						if r > 1 Then yer_atla_y = 5
						if AY = r Then
							Sh1.Cells(i+1,5 + yer_atla_y * (r -1)).Value = Sh1.Cells(i+1,5 +  yer_atla_y * (r -1)).Value + Ap_Oto_XX21.SF_MIKTAR(j) 
							Exit For
						End if
					Next
				End if
			Next
	Next
End Sub

Sub Satis_Ongoruleri(Sh1)

Dim OT_1L_2, AY_AL
s = 0
	Set OT_1L_2 = Doc.Select1("ot_han_2", "OTOSP1L", "ACIKKAPALI ='A' ", "", -1) '// cari ve grup kodu 1 e göre

	For k = 1 To 2
		s = s + 1
		VAR = Sh1.Cells(s,2).Value 
		if Trim(VAR) = "" Then Exit For
			For t = 1 To OT_1L_2.GetRecCount()
				if 	Trim(Sh1.Cells(s, 2).Value) = Trim(OT_1L_2.KOD(t)) Then 
					AY_AL = Doc.StrDate_Month(OT_1L_2.RTESTARIH(T))
					yer_atla_x = 0
					For r = 1 To 12				
						if r > 1 Then yer_atla_x = 5
						if AY_AL = r Then  Sh1.Cells(s ,4 + yer_atla_x * (r -1)).Value = Sh1.Cells(s ,4 + yer_atla_x * (r -1)).Value  +  OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(t)
					Next
					End if
			Next
	 	k = k -1
	Next

End Sub

Sub Kesin_Siparis(Sh1)
Dim	STOK40_T, ta_rih
	ta_rih = tarih_al&"/01/01"
 	Set STOK40_T= Doc.Select1("sdasd", "STOK40T", "REV_RTESTARIH >= '"&ta_rih&"'", "", -1)
s = 0
	For k = 1 To 2
		s = s + 1
		VAR = Sh1.Cells(s,2).Value 
		if Trim(VAR) = "" Then Exit For
			For t = 1 To STOK40_T.GetRecCount()
				if 	Trim(Sh1.Cells(s, 2).Value) = Trim(STOK40_T.KOD(t)) Then 
					AY_AL = Doc.StrDate_Month(STOK40_T.REV_RTESTARIH(t))
				yer_atla_a = 0	
				For r = 1 To 12
					if r > 1 Then yer_atla_a = 5
						if AY_AL = r Then  Sh1.Cells(s ,6 + yer_atla_a * (r -1)).Value = Sh1.Cells(s ,6 + yer_atla_a * (r -1)).Value + STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(t)
				Next
				End if
			Next
	 	k = k -1
	Next
End Sub

Sub Onceki_Yil_Gerceklesen(Sh1)

Dim	STOK70_T, tarih_tut, ta_rih, ta_rih_2 
	tarih_tut = tarih_al - 1
	ta_rih = tarih_tut&"/01/01"
	ta_rih_2 = tarih_tut&"/12/31"
	Set STOK70_T= Doc.Select1("545ewr", "STOK7XT", "TARIH >= '"&ta_rih&"' & TARIH <= '"&ta_rih_2&"'", "", -1)'/ STOK7XT YE GÖRE
s = 0
	if STOK70_T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
		For k = 1 To 2
			s = s + 1
			VAR = Sh1.Cells(s,2).Value 
			if Trim(VAR) = "" Then Exit For
				For t = 1 To STOK70_T.GetRecCount()
					if 	Trim(Sh1.Cells(s, 2).Value) = Trim(STOK70_T.KOD(t)) Then 
						AY_AL = Doc.StrDate_Month(STOK70_T.TARIH(t))
						yer_atla_p = 0	
						For r = 1 To 12
							if r > 1 Then yer_atla_p = 5
								if AY_AL = r Then  Sh1.Cells(s ,7 + yer_atla_p * (r -1)).Value = Sh1.Cells(s ,7 + yer_atla_p * (r -1)).Value  +  STOK70_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(t)
						Next	
					End if
				Next
	 		k = k -1
		Next
	End if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MAKO
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Alkor - Müþteri Öngörülerindem Satýþ Sipariþlerine Aktarma  (Önceki sipariþler kapanýyor) (14.05.12)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40G = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")
	Set OTOSP1_E = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E") 
	OTOSP1_E.ACIKKAPALI="K"
	For i = 1 to OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþler okunuyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		
			StokKodu = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
			TesTarih = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
			RevTesTarih = OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
			Miktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Programno = OTOSP1L.PROGRAM_NO(i)
			satir_no = OTOSP1L.REF_TEXT_205(i)
			
			OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
			ST40G.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40G.GetRecCount()
			ST40G.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo) = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
			ST40G.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
			ST40G.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			ST40G.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
			ST40G.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = RevTesTarih
			ST40G.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) =  Programno
			ST40G.REF_TEXT_205(satirNo) = satir_no

	Next
	
	Call SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40G)
	Call OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Doc.Save_Voucher

End Sub

Sub SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40)

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	
	Call OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set K_b1 = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = ST40.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	ST40E.FIYSEKLI = "DF"
	ST40E.CHSIPNO = ST40.PROGRAM_NO(1)
	
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþ yaratýlýyor : " & ST40E.EVRAKNO & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()
		
		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)		
		ST40T.CHSIPANO(satirNo) = ST40.REF_TEXT_205(i)

	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if

	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	if Len(Sipler) > 2 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Left(Sipler, Len(Sipler) - 2) & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
	end if
End Sub

Sub OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor..."
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("w2www", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' '&MUSTERIKODU='"+OTOSP1E.CH _KODU+"'&HOLDTOSEVK<>'E'", "", -1)
	if S40T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	S40T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Bulunan = 0
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Bulunan = 0
		Doc1.Load_Voucher S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor : " & S40T.EVRAKNO(i) & " - " & j & " / " & ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Bulunan = Bulunan + 1
			if ST40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(j) = 0 Then
				ST40T.KOD(j) = ""
				ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(j) = ""
				ST40T.RTESTARIH(j) = ""
			Else
				ST40T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = "K"
			End if
		Next
	Next
	if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Set OSP1E = Doc.Select1("dw466y4y", "OTOSP1E", "CH_KODU='" & OTOSP1E.CH_KODU & "'&ACIKKAPALI='A'&EVRAKNO<>'" & OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if OSP1E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("OTOSP1", "")
	Set O1E = Doc1.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	For i = 1 to OSP1E.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher OSP1E.EVRAKNO(i)
		O1E.ACIKKAPALI = "K"
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KODDUZELT
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kod Düzelt
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Kod = PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT
	KodArray = Split(Kod, vbcrlf)
	PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = ""
	Girinti = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
				
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = trim(PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		'  PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = YeniSatir
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " or trim(uCase(satir)) = "DO" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
				
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = trim(MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		'  MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = YeniSatir
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " or trim(uCase(satir)) = "DO" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Kod = MAKR8SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT
	KodArray = Split(Kod, vbcrlf)
	MAKR8SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = ""
	Girinti = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
				
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		MAKR8SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = trim(MAKR8SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		'  MAKR8SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = YeniSatir
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " or trim(uCase(satir)) = "DO" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MRP
Hangi Tablo için : STOKRC2
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MPS ÖNERÝLERÝNÝ VARDÝYA BAZINDA PARÇALARA BÖLME
Kod : 
PUBLIC M10E ,ii
Sub Makro1()
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	bos = ""
	
	for ii = 1 to M10E.GetRecCount()
		stokkodu = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(ii)
		ihtiyac_miktar = M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(ii)
		vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_MINIMUMSIPMIKTARI",stokkodu,"")
		if vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi = 0 or vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi = bos then vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi = ihtiyac_miktar
		
		proses_birimi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_6",stokkodu,"")  ' ilgili ürünün grupkodu-6 sýnda hangi üretim bölümü baz alýnarak bölüneceðine karar verilmesi
		vardiya_sayisi = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK6","AD_L2",proses_birimi,-1)  ' grup kodunun ad-2 alanýnda o bölümün vardiya sayýsý
		vardiya_sayisi = Doc.CINT(trim(vardiya_sayisi))

		if trim(vardiya_sayisi) = bos then vardiya_sayisi = 1

		oran = doc.div(1,vardiya_sayisi)   'vardiya sayýsýna baðlý olarak tarih revizyonu için kullanýyorum
		ihtiyac_tarihi = M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(ii)
		ihtiyac_tarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, -4) 	'sipariþler kaç gün önce hazýr olmalý
		M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(ii) = ihtiyac_tarihi
		
		mps_sayisi = Doc.Div(ihtiyac_miktar,vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi)
		mps_sayisi1 = Doc.Ceil(mps_sayisi)
		mps_sayisi2 = Doc.Floor(mps_sayisi)
		mps_miktari = vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi		'yarým kalmayan tam vardiya üretim miktarý
		mps_miktari2 = ihtiyac_miktar - (vardiya_uretim_kapasitesi * mps_sayisi2)		'vardiya üretim miktarý bazýnda son vardiyaya kalan küsüratlý üretim miktarý

		if mps_sayisi1 > 1 then

			fark_hsonu = 0

			ihtiyac_gunu_sayisi = doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(mps_sayisi1,vardiya_sayisi))
			uretilebilinecek_tarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, ihtiyac_gunu_sayisi)

			if doc.bugun < uretilebilinecek_tarih then
				ihtiyac_tarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(doc.bugun, (ihtiyac_gunu_sayisi+1))

				if haftasonu_mu(ihtiyac_tarihi) <> "HAYIR" Then
					ihtiyac_tarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, 1)
				End if
			End if

			for iii = 1 to mps_sayisi1
				fark = 1-(doc.ceil(iii * oran))

				if iii = 1 then
					uretilmesi_gereken_tarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, (fark - fark_hsonu))

					M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(ii) = uretilmesi_gereken_tarih

					M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(ii) = mps_miktari
					M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(ii) = mps_miktari
				else
					M10E.Addrow
					satir_git = M10E.GetRecCount()
					satirNo = M10E.GetRecCount() + 1
					M10E.CopyrowFromOtherTable satir_git, M10E, ii
					
					uretilmesi_gereken_tarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, (fark - fark_hsonu))

					if haftasonu_mu(uretilmesi_gereken_tarih) = "HAYIR" Then
						M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satir_git) = uretilmesi_gereken_tarih
					Else
						fark_hsonu = fark_hsonu + 1
						uretilmesi_gereken_tarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(ihtiyac_tarihi, (fark - fark_hsonu))
						M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satir_git) = uretilmesi_gereken_tarih
					End if

					if iii = mps_sayisi1 and mps_miktari2 > 0 then
						M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(satir_git) = mps_miktari2
						M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(satir_git) = mps_miktari2
					Else
						M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(satir_git) = mps_miktari
						M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(satir_git) = mps_miktari
					End if
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function haftasonu_mu(Tarihh)

	haftasonu_mu = "HAYIR"

	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarihh

	if d7Date.GetDOW() = 7 then
		haftasonu_mu = "EVET"
	End if
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32T_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(D7LB.JOBNO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Barkod operasyon barkodu deðil", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & d7LB.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox d7LB.JOBNO & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
		Set S32T = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "SFDC32T", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
		if S32T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if

		Call StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Else
		Call Cikar()
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	MTableT.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTableT.OPERASYON = MTable.OPERASYON
	MTableT.TEZGAH = MTable.TEZGAH
	MTableT.JOBNO = MTable.JOBNO

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & d7LB.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	MTable.MPSNO = D7LB.MPSNO
	MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM = D7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTable.JOBNO = D7LB.JOBNO
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON, "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU , "")

	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
'	MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO = SFDC32EvrakNo
'	MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM = SFDC32T_TRNum

	if trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) = "" or trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if UretimFisiniSil(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO, MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) <> 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GettableObject("SFDC32T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
			if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi iptal edildi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	Else
		Silindi = False
		For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
				SFDC32T.DeleteRowNoUI i
				Silindi = True
			End if
		Next
		if Silindi Then
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi kaydedildi.", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildirimiOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("sdsdfsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "", "JOBNO", -1)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.JOBNO(i) <> EskiIsNo Then
			Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
			Doc1.New_Voucher()
			SFDC32E.MPSNO = MTableT.MPSNO(i)
			SFDC32E.JOBNO = MTableT.JOBNO(i)
		End if
		EskiIsNo = MTableT.JOBNO(i)

		if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 and SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = 0 Then
			satirNo = 1
		Else
			satirNo = SFDC32T.AddRow()
		End if
		SFDC32T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SFDC32T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)

	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT1.Empty
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	if trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	SFDC32E.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SURE_KAYITLARI"
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
		Call EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
		Call SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, SFDC32E.MPSNO, SFDC32E.JOBNO)
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildirimi kaydedilemedi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	exit sub
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, MPSNo, IsNo)
	if Not SonOpMu(MPSNo, IsNo) Then Exit Sub

	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	SFDC32T.SafePack()
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20T = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Doc2.New_Voucher
	STOK20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
	
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = SFDC32E.MPSNO
		STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		STOK20T.KOD = SFDC32T.KOD(i)
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK20T.SERINO = SFDC32T.SERINO(i)
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK20T.W_FIRMA = Doc1.Getd7FirmCode()
		STOK20T.W_APP = Doc1.GetDocType()
		STOK20T.W_TABLE = "SFDC32T"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKTYPE = "S4"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKNO = SFDC32E.EVRAKNO
		STOK20T.W_TRNUM = SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)
	Next
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
	Doc2.Save_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher

'	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_URETIM_FISI"
'	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_URETIM_FISI_NOFORM"
'	Set STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI")
'	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.AMBCODE = "IM"
'	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
'	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"

'	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_URETIM_FISI2"

End Sub
Function SonOpMu(MPSNo, JobNo)
	SonOpMu = False
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", JobNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(SrNum) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("f23f23sdgh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonOpMu = True
	End if
End Function
Function UretimFisiniSil(EvrakNo, TrNum)
	SeriNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SFDC32T", "SERINO", EvrakNo & TrNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	QStr = "W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'&W_EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&W_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("sdfwegwhs", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		UretimFisiniSil = 0 
		Exit Function
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T1.EVRAKNO(1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then 
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
		Exit Function
	Else
		For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S20T.SERINO(i)) = trim(SeriNo) Then
				S20T.DeleRowNoUI i
			End if
		Next
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	End if
End Function
Sub EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)), 30)
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("adf3efsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB.SF_MIKTAR <> SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if Not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
					Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
				d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "LISTET"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT)

	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
'	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
'	Else
'		En = En - 24
'		Boy = Boy - 60
'	End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipari? Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Y?lem Yapmadan Çy&ky?" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'lary Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=30 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Açylacak MPS 'leri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "MPS 'leri Yarat" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSOlustur')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çyky?" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)

		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Else
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar1 = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar1
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 or Alan = "KOD" or Alan = "AD" Then
				if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
					AlanDegeri = LISTET.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				Else
					AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "CARI00")
					KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
					KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00", LISTET.KOD(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
				End if
				MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Doc.Setsaveable true
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "CARI00", MTableT.KOD(i))
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
'msgbox 111
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
				KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
				AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE" 
			End if
		Next 
'msgbox 112
	Next
	Doc.Setsaveable false
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='CARI00'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function

Sub Makro1()

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Surtel Ambalaj 'a Aktarma Makrosu (Silmeyin!!!!!)
Kod : 
Public HedefFirma
Public Const ALKKabloCHKodu = "4S003"  'ALK KON 'daki ALK Kablo C/H Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Call IrsaliyeAktar()
End Sub

Sub FaturaAktar()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK70E) Then Set STOK70E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK70T) Then Set STOK70T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	
	' if STOK70E.TARIH < "2013/01/13" Then Exit Sub
	gk9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","GK_9",STOK70E.CH_KODU,"")
	if trim(gk9) <> "KONSINYE" Then Exit Sub
	
	STOK70T.SafePack()
	if STOK70T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK24", "", HedefFirma)
	if Doc1 is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda Maliyetli Stok Düzeltme Fiþi evraký yaratýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")
	
	if KodEksik2(Doc1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK70E.EVRAKNO
	' S24E.CH_KODU = ALKKabloCHKodu
	Depo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1",STOK70E.CH_KODU,"")
	
	S24E.AMBCODE = trim(Depo)
	S24E.TARIH = STOK70E.TARIH
	S24E.XNITELIK0 = STOK70E.GK_3
	
	' if TRIM(STOK70E.NITELIK)="XK" then
	'  S24E.NITELIK = "Z1"
	' else
	'  S24E.NITELIK = STOK70E.NITELIK
	' end if
	
	' if trim(STOK70E.CH_KURV) <> ""  and trim(STOK70E.CH_KUR) then
	'  S24E.CH_KUR = STOK70E.CH_KUR
	'  S24E.CH_KURV = STOK70E.CH_KURV
	' End if
	
	S24T.Empty
	For i = 1 to STOK70T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S24T.AddRow()
		S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S24T.KOD(satirNo) = STOK70T.KOD(i)
		S24T.TRNUM(satirNo) = STOK70T.TRNUM(i)
		S24T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		'  S24T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S24T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S24T.SERINO(satirNo) = STOK70T.SERINO(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_VR_C(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		S24T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		
		S24T.GIREN_OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = STOK70T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i)
		
		S24T.PUNIT_ORG(satirNo) = STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(i)
		S24T.KUR(satirNo) = STOK70T.KUR(i)
		
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " Firmasýna Aktarýldý " & STOK70E.EVRAKNO, "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
End Sub
Function KodEksik2(Doc1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	KodEksik2 = False
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2G("df234gsdh", "STOK70T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		Set S00 = Doc1.Select1("fgerhgdeh", "STOK00", "KOD='" & S70T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if StokKoduAktar(S70T.KOD(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(S70T.KOD(i)) & " stok kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK
				KodEksik2 = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Function KodEksik3(Doc1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	KodEksik3 = False
	Set C20T = Doc.Select2G("SADf2S4gsdh", "CARI20T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to C20T.GetRowCount()
		Set C00 = Doc1.Select1("fgerhgdeh", "CARI00", "KOD='" & C20T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if C00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if CariKoduAktar(C20T.KOD(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(C20T.KOD(i)) & " Cari hesap kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK
				KodEksik3 = True
			End if
		End if
		
		Set M00 = Doc1.Select1("fgR1T32", "MUHA00", "KOD='" & C20T.MUHASEBECODE(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if M00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if MuhasebeKoduAktar(C20T.MUHASEBECODE(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(C20T.MUHASEBECODE(i)) & " Muhasebe hesap kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK
				KodEksik3 = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub IrsaliyeAktar()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60E) Then Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60T) Then Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	
	if STOK60E.TARIH < "2011/04/01" Then Exit Sub
	
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	
	if trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "1S082" and trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "4S008" Then Exit Sub
	
	if trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye Sýra No alaný doldurulmamýþ. Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlmadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	STOK60T.SafePack()
	if STOK60T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	if STOK60T.SIPNO(1) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ baðlantýsý yapýlmamýþ. Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlmadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	SipCHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", STOK60T.SIPNO(1), "")
	
	' ALKKabloCHKodu = "4S003"
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK60", "", HedefFirma)
	if Doc1 is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda irsaliye evraký yaratýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set S60E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	
	if KodEksik(Doc1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO
	S60E.CH_KODU = SipCHKodu
	S60E.AMBCODE = STOK60E.AMBCODE
	S60E.TARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
	
	S60T.Empty
	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S60T.AddRow()
		S60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S60T.KOD(satirNo) = STOK60T.KOD(i)
		S60T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = STOK60T.TRNUM(i)
		S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S60T.NOTES(satirNo) = STOK60T.SERINO(i)
		
		'  S60T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		'  Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T", "SF_MIKTAR", satirNo
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " Firmasýna Aktarýldý", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
End Sub
Function KodEksik(Doc1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	KodEksik = False
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("df234gsdh", "STOK60T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		Set S00 = Doc1.Select1("fgerhgdeh", "STOK00", "KOD='" & S60T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if StokKoduAktar(S60T.KOD(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(S60T.KOD(i)) & " stok kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK
				KodEksik = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function StokKoduAktar(StokKodu)
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK00", "", HedefFirma)
	Set S00_1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00_2 = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	
	Doc2.Load_Voucher TRIM(StokKodu)
	Doc1.Load_Voucher TRIM(StokKodu)
	S00_1.AD = S00_2.AD
	S00_1.IUNIT = S00_2.IUNIT
	S00_1.CODETYPE = "D"
	S00_1.GK_9 = S00_2.GK_9
	
	StokKoduAktar = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function CariKoduAktar(cariKod)
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("CARI00", "", HedefFirma)
	Set C00_1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI00")
	
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("CARI00", "")
	Set C00_2 = Doc2.GetTableObject("CARI00")
	
	Doc2.Load_Voucher TRIM(cariKod)
	Doc1.Load_Voucher TRIM(cariKod)
	C00_1.AD = C00_2.AD
	C00_1.IUNIT = C00_2.IUNIT
	C00_1.CODETYPE = "D"
	C00_1.GK_9 = C00_2.GK_9
	
	CariKoduAktar = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function MuhasebeKoduAktar(MuhasebeKodu)
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("MUHA00", "", HedefFirma)
	Set M00_1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA00")
	
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA00", "")
	Set M00_2 = Doc2.GetTableObject("MUHA00")
	
	Doc2.Load_Voucher TRIM(MuhasebeKodu)
	Doc1.Load_Voucher TRIM(MuhasebeKodu)
	M00_1.AD = M00_2.AD
	M00_1.IUNIT = M00_2.IUNIT
	M00_1.CODETYPE = "D"
	M00_1.GK_9 = M00_2.GK_9
	
	MuhasebeKoduAktar = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function

Function ALKKONIrsaliyeFaturalanmis()
	ALKKONIrsaliyeFaturalanmis = False
	
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60E) Then Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60T) Then Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	
	if trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "1S082" and trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "4S008" Then Exit Function
	
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK60", "", HedefFirma)
	
	IrsaliyeNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO), 10)
	Set S60T = Doc1.Select1("sdf34fwerg", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='" & IrsaliyeNo & "'&SF_FATURALANANMIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if S60T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		ALKKONIrsaliyeFaturalanmis = True
	End if
End Function
Sub SetHedefFirma()
	HedefFirma = Replace(trim(Doc.Getd7FirmCode), "ALK", "KON")
'	HedefFirma = "ALK_2009"
End Sub

Sub Cariislemfisi()
	if Doc.IsBlank(CARI20E) Then Set CARI20E = Doc.GetTableObject("CARI20E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(CARI20T) Then Set CARI20T = Doc.GetTableObject("CARI20T")
	
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	msgbox 111
	
	CARI20T.SafePack()
	if CARI20T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("CARI20", "", HedefFirma)
	if Doc1 is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda Cari Hesap Ýþlem Fiþi evraký yaratýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Set C20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI20E")
	Set C20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("CARI20T")
	
	if KodEksik3(Doc1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher CARI20E.EVRAKNO
	C20E.TARIH = CARI20E.TARIH
	C20E.GK_3 = CARI20E.GK_3
		
	C20T.Empty
	msgbox 112
	For i = 1 to CARI20T.GetRowCount()
		
		gk9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","GK_9",CARI20T.KOD(i),"")
		if trim(gk9) <> "KONSINYE" Then Exit Sub

		satirNo = C20T.AddRow()
		C20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
		C20T.EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = CARI20T.EVRAKTYPE(i)
		C20T.TRNUM(satirNo) = CARI20T.TRNUM(i)
		C20T.SRNUM(satirNo) = CARI20T.SRNUM(i)
		C20T.KOD(satirNo) = CARI20T.KOD(i)
		C20T.TARIH(satirNo) = CARI20T.TARIH(i)
		C20T.NITELIK(satirNo) = CARI20T.NITELIK(i)
		C20T.CHXNITELIK2(satirNo) = CARI20T.CHXNITELIK2(i)
		C20T.CHXNITELIK3(satirNo) = CARI20T.CHXNITELIK3(i)
		C20T.EXTERNALEVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = CARI20T.EXTERNALEVRAKTYPE(i)
		C20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = CARI20T.ACIKLAMA(i)
		C20T.ACIKLAMA2(satirNo) = CARI20T.ACIKLAMA2(i)
		C20T.ACIKLAMA3(satirNo) = CARI20T.ACIKLAMA3(i)
		C20T.TL_BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = CARI20T.TL_BORCFIYAT(i)
		C20T.TL_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = CARI20T.TL_ALACAKFIYAT(i)
		C20T.VALORORANI(satirNo) = CARI20T.VALORORANI(i)
		C20T.VALORTARIH(satirNo) = CARI20T.VALORTARIH(i)
		C20T.ONAYEH(satirNo) = CARI20T.ONAYEH(i)
		C20T.MFORCE(satirNo) = CARI20T.MFORCE(i)
		C20T.MUHASEBECODE(satirNo) = CARI20T.MUHASEBECODE(i)
		C20T.MUHASEBECODE2(satirNo) = CARI20T.MUHASEBECODE2(i)
		C20T.CH_BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_BORCFIYAT(i)
		C20T.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_ALACAKFIYAT(i)
		C20T.CH_KUR(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_KUR(i)
		C20T.KDV19(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV19(i)
		C20T.KDV39(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV39(i)
		C20T.TLOG_USERNAME(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_USERNAME(i)
		C20T.TLOG_LOGTARIH(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_LOGTARIH(i)
		C20T.TLOG_LOGTIME(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_LOGTIME(i)
		C20T.TLOG_PSTATION(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_PSTATION(i)
		C20T.KARSI_CODE(satirNo) = CARI20T.KARSI_CODE(i)
		C20T.CH_KURV(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_KURV(i)
		C20T.ISLEMTARIHI(satirNo) = CARI20T.ISLEMTARIHI(i)
		C20T.KURCALCTYPEANDEXT(satirNo) = CARI20T.KURCALCTYPEANDEXT(i)
		C20T.CRHDST_STATU(satirNo) = CARI20T.CRHDST_STATU(i)
		C20T.CRHDST_STATU_K(satirNo) = CARI20T.CRHDST_STATU_K(i)
		C20T.TLOG_APP(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_APP(i)
		C20T.TLOG_BAPP(satirNo) = CARI20T.TLOG_BAPP(i)
		C20T.KDV191(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV191(i)
		C20T.KDV192(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV192(i)
		C20T.KDV391(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV391(i)
		C20T.KDV392(satirNo) = CARI20T.KDV392(i)
		C20T.GK_1(satirNo) = CARI20T.GK_1(i)
		C20T.GK_2(satirNo) = CARI20T.GK_2(i)
		C20T.GK_3(satirNo) = CARI20T.GK_3(i)
		C20T.TRTYPE(satirNo) = CARI20T.TRTYPE(i)
		C20T.OR_EVRAKSERINO(satirNo) = CARI20T.OR_EVRAKSERINO(i)
		C20T.OR_EVRAKSIRANO(satirNo) = CARI20T.OR_EVRAKSIRANO(i)
		C20T.OR_EVRAKTARIHI(satirNo) = CARI20T.OR_EVRAKTARIHI(i)
		C20T.MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI(satirNo) = CARI20T.MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI(i)
		C20T.MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR(satirNo) = CARI20T.MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR(i)
		C20T.CH_UNVAN(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_UNVAN(i)
		C20T.CH_VERGID(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_VERGID(i)
		C20T.CH_VERGINO(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_VERGINO(i)
		C20T.CH_TCNO(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_TCNO(i)
		C20T.CH_EBYN_ULKE(satirNo) = CARI20T.CH_EBYN_ULKE(i)
		C20T.BABS_DAHILDEGIL(satirNo) = CARI20T.BABS_DAHILDEGIL(i)
		C20T.EXTERNALEVRAKNO(satirNo) = CARI20T.EXTERNALEVRAKNO(i)
		C20T.GGBNO(satirNo) = CARI20T.GGBNO(i)
		C20T.GGBTARIHI(satirNo) = CARI20T.GGBTARIHI(i)
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " Firmasýna Aktarýldý" & CARI20E.EVRAKNO, "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
msgbox 22999
End Sub
