Kod : 
Public tezgahno, MTable 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32T_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_SAAT","CHAR",5,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_TARIH2","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SFDC32_SAAT2","CHAR",5,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableD=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_DURUS=STACK","IO")
'	MTableD.Addfield2 "DURMA_SEBEBI","CHAR",4,0,"R","","DURMAS"
	MTableD.Addfield2 "DURMA_SEBEBI","CHAR",4,0,"R","",""
	MTableD.Addfield2 "SURE","DOUBLE",16,0,"R","",""

	Set MTableF=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_FIRE=STACK","IO")
	MTableF.Addfield2 "SEBEP","CHAR",4,0,"R","",""
	MTableF.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,2,"R","",""

	Set MTableO=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_OPR=STACK","IO")
	MTableO.Addfield2 "OPERATOR","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set MTableT4 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_OPR")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	if trim(Barkod) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if Left(Barkod, 4) <> "%JB%" and Left(Barkod, 4) <> "%TZ%" and Left(Barkod, 4) <> "%OP%" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Barkod operasyon veya Tezgah barkodu deðil", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then 

		IsNo = Doc.RightJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), 5), 16)
		Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
		if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
	'		Doc.d7MsgBox d7LB.JOBNO & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
			Doc.d7MsgBox M10T.JOBNO & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub		
		End if

	'	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
			Call StokBilgileriniDoldur(IsNo)
	'	End if
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
	Elseif Left(Barkod, 4) = "%TZ%" then
		tezgahno = Doc.RightJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), 5), 24)
		MTable.TEZGAH = tezgahno
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	Elseif Left(Barkod, 4) = "%OP%" then
		operatorS = Doc.RightJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), 5), 16)
		MTableT4.OPERATOR = operatorS
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	End if
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub EkleCikar()
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
		Call Ekle()
	Else
		Call Cikar()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
'	Call CalcOpMiktar(MTable.MPSNO, MTable.JOBNO, BuOpToplamMiktar, OncekiOpMiktar)
'	msgbox BuOpToplamMiktar & ", " & OncekiOpMiktar
'	if BuOpToplamMiktar > OncekiOpMiktar and OncekiOpMiktar <> -1 Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Operasyonun Tamamlanan Miktarý  :" & OncekiOpMiktar & vbcrlf & "Bu operasyonun Tamamlanan miktarý : " & BuOpToplamMiktar & vbcrlf & "giriþ yapýlamaz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
'		Exit Sub		
'	End if

	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	MTableT.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTableT.OPERASYON = MTable.OPERASYON
	MTableT.TEZGAH = MTable.TEZGAH
	MTableT.JOBNO = MTable.JOBNO

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	IsNo = MTable.JOBNO
	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		if S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") < STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Daha önce okutulan miktardan fazla çýkýlamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			STOK01_PDA.Empty
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub
		End if
		KalanMiktar = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if trim(MTableT.JOBNO(i)) = trim(IsNo) Then
				if KalanMiktar <= MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
					CikilacakMiktar = KalanMiktar
				Else
					CikilacakMiktar = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				End if
				MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) - CikilacakMiktar
				if MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) = 0 Then
					MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
					MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				End if
				KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - CikilacakMiktar
				if KalanMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.SafePack()
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur(IsNo)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.SetFieldValueNV "BARKOD", -1, ""

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdfwgsdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("sddddfwgsdg", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= M10T.R_MAMULKODU
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_SF_UNIT", M10T.EVRAKNO, "")

	MTable.MPSNO = M10T.EVRAKNO
	MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM
	MTable.JOBNO = IsNo
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON, "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU
	MTable.TEZGAH = ""
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU , "")

	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
'	MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO = SFDC32EvrakNo
'	MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM = SFDC32T_TRNum

	if trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) = "" or trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if UretimFisiniSil(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO, MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) <> 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GettableObject("SFDC32T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
			if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi iptal edildi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	Else
		Silindi = False
		For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)) = trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) Then
				SFDC32T.DeleteRowNoUI i
				Silindi = True
			End if
		Next
		if Silindi Then
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Çalýþma Bildirimi kaydedildi.", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SFDC32_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildiriminde " & vbcrlf & _
								trim(MTable.SFDC32T_TRNUM) & " no.lu satýr bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Ýptal", MB_OK
			MTable.Empty		
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildirimiOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_DURUS")
	Set MTableT3 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_FIRE")
	Set MTableT4 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_OPR")

	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("sdsdfsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "", "JOBNO", -1)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")
	Set SFDC32D = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32D")
	Set SFDC20S = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC20S")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")


	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.JOBNO(i) <> EskiIsNo Then
			Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
			Doc1.New_Voucher()
			SFDC32E.MPSNO = MTableT.MPSNO(i)
			SFDC32E.JOBNO = MTableT.JOBNO(i)

			SFDC32E.RECTARIH1=MTable.SFDC32_TARIH
			SFDC32E.RECTIME1=MTable.SFDC32_SAAT
			SFDC32E.RECTARIH2=MTable.SFDC32_TARIH2
			SFDC32E.RECTIME2=MTable.SFDC32_SAAT2

		End if
		EskiIsNo = MTableT.JOBNO(i)

		if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 1 and SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = 0 Then
			satirNo = 1
		Else
			satirNo = SFDC32T.AddRow()
		End if
		SFDC32T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SFDC32T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		satirNo_D = SFDC32D.AddRow()
		SFDC32D.DURMA_SEBEBI(satirNo_D) = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTableT2.DURMA_SEBEBI(1)),4)
		SFDC32D.SURE(satirNo_D) = Doc.Div(trim(MTableT2.SURE(1)),60)
		bos = ""

		satirNo_O = SFDC32O.AddRow()
		SFDC32O.OPERATOR(satirNo_O) = MTableT4.OPERATOR

		if trim(MTableT3.SF_MIKTAR(1)) <> bos then
			satirNo_F = SFDC20S.AddRow()
			urunkodu = doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10E","MAMULSTOKKODU",MTableT.MPSNO(i),"")
			SFDC20S.SEBEP(satirNo_F) = "01"
			SFDC20S.KOD(satirNo_F) = urunkodu
			SFDC20S.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo_F) = MTableT3.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		End if
	Next
	Call CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)

	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT1.Empty
	MTableT2.Empty
	MTableT3.Empty
	MTableT4.Empty
End Sub
Sub CalismaBildiriminiKaydet(Doc1)
	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	if trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 Then
			SFDC32T.KOD(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
			SFDC32T.RECETEKOD(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	SFDC32E.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SURE_KAYITLARI"
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
'		Call EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
		Call SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, SFDC32E.MPSNO, SFDC32E.JOBNO)
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu çalýþma bildirimi kaydedilemedi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	exit sub
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub SonOpIseUretimFisiKes(Doc1, MPSNo, IsNo)
	if Not SonOpMu(MPSNo, IsNo) Then Exit Sub

	Set SFDC32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")

	SFDC32T.SafePack()
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20T = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Doc2.New_Voucher

	if left(SFDC32T.KOD(1),3) = "AOS" or left(SFDC32T.KOD(1),3) = "HTT" or left(SFDC32T.KOD(1),3) = "FKS" then
		STOK20E.AMBCODE = "0"
	else
		STOK20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
	End if

	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
	
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = SFDC32E.MPSNO
		STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		STOK20T.KOD = SFDC32T.KOD(i)
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = SFDC32T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK20T.SERINO = SFDC32T.SERINO(i)
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK20T.W_FIRMA = Doc1.Getd7FirmCode()
		STOK20T.W_APP = Doc1.GetDocType()
		STOK20T.W_TABLE = "SFDC32T"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKTYPE = "S4"
		STOK20T.W_EVRAKNO = SFDC32E.EVRAKNO
		STOK20T.W_TRNUM = SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)
	Next
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_006"
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
	Doc2.Save_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function SonOpMu(MPSNo, JobNo)
	SonOpMu = False
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", JobNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(SrNum) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("f23f23sdgh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonOpMu = True
	End if
End Function
Function UretimFisiniSil(EvrakNo, TrNum)
	SeriNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SFDC32T", "SERINO", EvrakNo & TrNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	QStr = "W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'&W_EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&W_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("sdfwegwhs", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		UretimFisiniSil = 0 
		Exit Function
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T1.EVRAKNO(1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then 
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
		Exit Function
	Else
		For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S20T.SERINO(i)) = trim(SeriNo) Then
				S20T.DeleRowNoUI i
			End if
		Next
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	End if
End Function
Sub EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)), 30)
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("adf3efsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB.SF_MIKTAR <> SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if Not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
					Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
				d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub CalcOpMiktar(MPSNo, IsNo, BuOpTamamlananMiktar, OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sd2g24sdfgdsg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM<'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = -1
	Else
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(1)
	End if
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("dfw34gsg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = 0
	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = MTableT.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	BuOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO") + BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar + _
						STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
 End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : URETIMFISI
Hangi Tablo için : SFDC32
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : LÝSTE TABINDAKÝ ÇALIÞMA BÝLDÝRÝMLERÝNÝN YARI MAMUL ÜRETÝM FÝÞLERÝNÝ OLUÞTUR
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		
		stokkodu = LISTET.STOK_CODE(i)
		aksiyon = LISTET.D7_AKSIYON(i)
		mps = LISTET.MPSNO(i)
		miktar = LISTET.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		gk8 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_8",stokkodu,"")
		if trim(gk8) <> "MM" then
			Call Uretim_fisi_olustur(stokkodu, aksiyon, mps, job, miktar)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Uretim_fisi_olustur(stokkodu,aksiyon,mps,job,miktar)
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20","")
	Set S20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher

	S20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
	S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
	S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "NK"

'	S20E.AMBCODE = "1MON3"
'	S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "1MON3"
'	S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "1HRD"

	S20T.AddRow
	satirNo = S20T.GetRecCount
	S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	
	S20T.MPSNO = mps
	S20T.REF_TEXT_1 = aksiyon
	S20T.KOD = stokkodu
	S20T.SF_MIKTAR = CDBL(miktar)

	Set S83T = Doc.Select1("sdfs32","STOK83T","MPSNO = '"& mps &"'","",-1)
	if S83T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		S20T.LOTNUMBER = S83T.LOTNUMBER(1)
	End if

	S20T.SafePack

	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Açýklama : AÇIK MPS'LERÝN TEZGAH BAZINDA HAFTALIK MIK. (Haftalýk Ön Ýmalat ve Montaj Programý) - 11.04.2012
Kod : 
Public Sanal_M10T
Sub Makro1() 
	
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)

	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = "AÇIK MPS'LERÝN TEZGAH BAZINDA HAFTALIK MIKTARLARI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,1).Value = "TEZGAH GRUP KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,2).Value = "TEZGAH ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,3).Value = "STOK KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,4).Value = "STOK ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,5).Value = "HAFTA1"
	Sh1.Cells(3,6).Value = "HAFTA2"
	Sh1.Cells(3,7).Value = "HAFTA3"
	Sh1.Cells(3,8).Value = "HAFTA4"
	Sh1.Cells(3,9).Value = "HAFTA5"
	Sh1.Cells(3,10).Value = "HAFTA6"
	Sh1.Cells(3,11).Value = "ÝÞ EMÝRÝ"

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(3,1),Sh1.Cells(3,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge

	Excel.Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 19
	Excel.Columns(2).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(3).ColumnWidth = 22
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 50
	Excel.Columns(5).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(6).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(7).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(8).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(9).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(10).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(11).ColumnWidth = 20

	HAFTA1 = 0
	HAFTA2 = 0
	HAFTA3 = 0
	HAFTA4 = 0
	HAFTA5 = 0
	HAFTA6 = 0

	satir = 4
	Set mpstarihi=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	Set bugun=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	bos = ""
	mpsno = ""
	mpsmiktari = 0
	satirNo = 1

	Call CreateTable()

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("Ssda23","MMPS10T","ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"' & R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)

	if M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to M10T.GetRecCount()

'		if kk = 100 then 
'			exit for
'		End if

			satirNo = Sanal_M10T.AddRow()
			Sanal_M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

'			bugun.SetDate("13/04/2012")
			bugun.SetDate(doc.bugun)
			buhafta = bugun.GetWOY_ISO()

			mpsno = M10T.EVRAKNO(kk)
			M_REV_TAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E","REVURETIMDENTESTARIH",mpsno,"")
'*************************************************
'			M_REV_TAR = Doc.StrDate_Increment(M_REV_TAR, 1)
'*************************************************
			mpstarihi.SetDate(M_REV_TAR)
			hafta = mpstarihi.GetWOY_ISO()

'			mpsmiktari = M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(kk)
			mpsmiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E","SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR",mpsno,"")

			tezgahkodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(kk)
			tezgahadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgk1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_1",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgkadi = Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK1","AD",tezgahgk1,-1)

			tezgahtekrarsayisi = 0
			Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("S0s0da23",M10T.GetAppName,"ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"' & R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'& EVRAKNO = '"& mpsno &"'","",-1)

			if M10T2.GetRecCount() > 0 then
				for kl = 1 to M10T2.GetRecCount()
					if M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(kk) = M10T2.R_KAYNAKKODU(kl) then
						tezgahtekrarsayisi = tezgahtekrarsayisi + 1
					End if
				Next
			End if
			mpsmiktari = Doc.div(mpsmiktari,tezgahtekrarsayisi)

			stokkodu = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(kk)
			stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")

			fark = hafta - buhafta
			if fark <= 0 then
				fark = 0
			End if
			
			Select Case fark
			Case "0"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_1 = mpsmiktari
				Sanal_M10T.MPS_NO = mpsno
			Case "1"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_2 = mpsmiktari
			Case "2"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_3 = mpsmiktari
			Case "3"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_4 = mpsmiktari
			Case "4"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_5 = mpsmiktari
			Case "5"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_6 = mpsmiktari
			End Select
		
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD = trim(tezgahgkadi)
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD = trim(tezgahadi)
			Sanal_M10T.KOD = trim(stokkodu)
			Sanal_M10T.AD = trim(stokadi)
		Next
	End if

'	Sanal_M10T.Sort("HAFTA_1")

'	Sanal_M10T.GROUPBY("TZ_KOD+KOD+MPS_NO")  '////tercih
	Sanal_M10T.GROUPBY("TZ_KOD+KOD")

	if Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for ii = 1 to Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount()

			Sh1.Cells(satir,1).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,2).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,3).Value = Sanal_M10T.KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,4).Value = Sanal_M10T.AD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,5).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_1(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,6).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_2(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,7).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_3(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,8).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_4(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,9).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_5(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,10).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_6(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,11).Value = Sanal_M10T.MPS_NO(ii)

			satir = satir + 1
		next
	End if
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Sub CreateTable()
	Set Sanal_M10T = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_GK_KOD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_KOD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_1","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_2","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_3","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_4","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_5","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_6","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "MPS_NO","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Empty()
	Sanal_M10T.AddReportfields
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	bos = ""
	sipno = 3407

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set S40 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	
	Set S40T = Doc1.Select1("dsfs","STOK40T","EVRAKNO = '"& sipno &"' & ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2 = '"& BOS &"'","",-1)
	if S40T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		
		for i = 1 to S40T.GetRecCount()
			Siparis = S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
			artikel = S40T.ARTNO(i)
			Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dsfs","MMPS10TM","MUSTERISIPIARTNO = '"& S40T.ARTNO(i) &"'","",-1)
			if M10TM.GetRecCount() > 0 then
				mpsno = M10TM.EVRAKNO(1)
				
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Siparis
				
				for k = 1 to S40.GetRecCount()
					if S40.ARTNO(k) = artikel then
						S40.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(k) = mpsno
					End if
				Next
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	'0000000345
	'kalan bakiye hesabý yaparken negatif kontrolü olacakmý olmayacakmý
	
	
	Call ithalatkontrol()
End Sub
Sub ithalatkontrol()
	
	Set S46T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	
	GK9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","GK_9",STOK46E.CH_KODU,"")
	
	if trim(GK9) = "ITHALAT" then
		For i = 1 to S46T.GetRowCount()
			GTP = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GTIP",S46T.KOD(i),"")
			if trim(gtp) <> "" and trim(S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i)) = "" then
				
				QStr = "SELECT STOK19T.* FROM STOK19T"
				QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK19E ON STOK19T.EVRAKNO=STOK19E.EVRAKNO"
				QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK19E.GK_2<>'MASTER    '"
				QStr = QStr & " AND STOK19T.KOD='"& S46T.KOD(i) &"'"
				
				Set S19T2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrg35h35hgtfhg", QStr)
				
				S19T2.Groupby("EVRAKNO+KOD")
				
				if S19T2.GetRecCount() > 0 then
					if S19T2.GetRowCount() = 1 then
						sipno = S46T.EVRAKNO(1)
						tarih = S46T.TARIH(1)
						serbestmiktar = Doc.Div(S19T2.SF_MIKTAR(1) * 10,100)
						if S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) > serbestmiktar then
							msgbox "yeterli ithalat kotanýz yok"
						Else
							aciklama = S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_7(i)
							listeno = S19T2.EVRAKNO(1)
							ukod = S19T2.KOD(1)
							miktar = S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
							call MalzemeListesineYaz(listeno,ukod,miktar,sipno,aciklama,tarih)
							S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i) = listeno
							Doc.StatusbarMessage "DÝÝB güncelleniyor... : " & Listeno
						End if
					Else
						For k = 1 to S19T2.GetRowCount()
							sipno = S46T.EVRAKNO(k)
							tarih = S46T.TARIH(k)
							serbestmiktar = Doc.Div(S19T2.SF_MIKTAR(k) * 10,100)
							
							if S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i) > serbestmiktar then
								if k = S19T2.GetRowCount() then
									msgbox "yeterli ithalat kotanýz yok"
								End if
							Else
								aciklama = S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_7(i)
								listeno = S19T2.EVRAKNO(k)
								ukod = S19T2.KOD(k)
								miktar = S46T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
								call MalzemeListesineYaz(listeno,ukod,miktar,sipno,aciklama,tarih)
								S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_8(i) = listeno
								Doc.StatusbarMessage "DÝÝB güncelleniyor... : " & Listeno & " - " & k & " / " & S19T2.GetRecCount()
								exit for
							End if
						Next
					End if
				End if
			End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub
Sub MalzemeListesineYaz(listeno,ukod,miktar,sipno,aciklama,tarih)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK19", "")
	Set S19E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK19E")
	Set S19T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK19T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher listeno
	
	for ii = 1 to S19T.GetRecCount()
		if S19T.KOD(ii) = ukod then
			
			if S19T.SF_MIKTAR(ii) > 0 and ii <> S19T.GetRecCount() then
				if S19T.KOD(ii) = S19T.KOD(ii+1) Then
					For j = ii to S19T.GetRecCount()
						if j = S19T.GetRecCount() then
							ii = j
						else
							if S19T.KOD(j) <> S19T.KOD(j+1) Then
								ii = j
								exit for
							End if
						End if
					Next
				End if
			End if
			if ii => S19T.GetRecCount() Then
				S19T.AddRow
				satirno = S19T.GetRecCount()
				S19T.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
			Else
				S19T.InsertRow(ii+1)
				S19T.SetCurrentRow(ii + 1)
			End if
			
			S19T.REF_NUM_6(ii + 1) = S19T.REF_NUM_6(ii)
			S19T.KOD(ii + 1) = S19T.KOD(ii)
			S19T.REF_NUM_2(ii + 1) = miktar
			if S19T.REF_NUM_1(ii) <> 0 then
				S19T.REF_NUM_1(ii + 1) = S19T.REF_NUM_1(ii) - miktar
			else
				S19T.REF_NUM_1(ii + 1) = S19T.SF_MIKTAR(ii) - miktar
			End if
			S19T.REF_TARIH_1(ii + 1) = doc.strtonordate(tarih)
			S19T.REF_TEXT_1(ii + 1) = trim(sipno)
			S19T.REF_TEXT_2(ii + 1) = trim(aciklama)
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			ii = ii + 1
		End if
	Next
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : AK-SP
Hangi Tablo için : OTOSP1
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Alkor - Müþteri Öngörülerindem Satýþ Sipariþlerine Aktarma  (Önceki sipariþler kapanýyor) (14.05.12)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40G = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")
	Set OTOSP1_E = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E") 
	OTOSP1_E.ACIKKAPALI="K"

	For i = 1 to OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþler okunuyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRecCount()
		
			StokKodu = OTOSP1L.KOD(i)
			TesTarih = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
			RevTesTarih = OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
			Miktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Programno = OTOSP1L.PROGRAM_NO(i)
			satir_no = OTOSP1L.REF_TEXT_205(i)
			
			OTOSP1L.ACIKKAPALI2(i) = "K"
			ST40G.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40G.GetRecCount()
			ST40G.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo) = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
			ST40G.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
			ST40G.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			ST40G.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TesTarih
			ST40G.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = RevTesTarih
			ST40G.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) =  Programno
			ST40G.REF_TEXT_205(satirNo) = satir_no

	Next
	
	Call SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40G)
	Call OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Doc.Save_Voucher

End Sub

Sub SevkEdilecekleriAktar(ST40)

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	
	Call OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set K_b1 = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU = ST40.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	ST40E.FIYSEKLI = "DF"
	ST40E.CHSIPNO = ST40.PROGRAM_NO(1)
	
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sipariþ yaratýlýyor : " & ST40E.EVRAKNO & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()
		
		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)		
		ST40T.CHSIPANO(satirNo) = ST40.REF_TEXT_205(i)

	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if

	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	if Len(Sipler) > 2 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Left(Sipler, Len(Sipler) - 2) & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK+MB_ICONINFORMATION)
	end if
End Sub

Sub OncekiSiparisleriKapat()
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor..."

	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("ghjkkl", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' ' & MUSTERIKODU='"+OTOSP1E.CH_KODU+"' & HOLDTOSEVK <> 'E'", "", -1)

	if S40T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	S40T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Bulunan = 0
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Bulunan = 0
		Doc1.Load_Voucher S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki sipariþler kapatýlýyor : " & S40T.EVRAKNO(i) & " - " & j & " / " & ST40T.GetRowCount()
			Bulunan = Bulunan + 1

			if ST40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(j) = 0 Then
				ST40T.KOD(j) = ""
				ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(j) = ""
				ST40T.RTESTARIH(j) = ""
			Else
				ST40T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = "K"
			End if
		Next
	Next
	if Bulunan > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub OncekiOngoruleriKapat()
	Set OSP1E = Doc.Select1("dw466y4y", "OTOSP1E", "CH_KODU='" & OTOSP1E.CH_KODU & "'&ACIKKAPALI='A'&EVRAKNO<>'" & OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if OSP1E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("OTOSP1", "")
	Set O1E = Doc1.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	For i = 1 to OSP1E.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher OSP1E.EVRAKNO(i)
		O1E.ACIKKAPALI = "K"
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set KriterTbl = Doc.CreateTableObject("KRITERLER=STACK","IO")
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "YM_KONTROL","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "SKODB","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "SKODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_1B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK1"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_1E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK1"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_2B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK2"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_2E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK2"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_3B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK3"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_3E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK3"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_4B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK4"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_4E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK4"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_5B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK5"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_5E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK5"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_6B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK6"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_6E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK6"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_7B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK7"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_7E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK7"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_8B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK8"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_8E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK8"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_9B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK9"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_9E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGK9"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_10B","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGKA"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_10E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGKA"

	Set HOZET = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HMD_OZET=STACK","IO")
	HOZET.Addfield2 "R_KAYNAKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_IHTIYAC","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "REZERVE_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "SERBEST_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "ILK_IHTIYAC_TARIHI","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "ILK_TALEP_TARIHI","DATE",10,0,"L","",""

	Set HLIST = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HMD_LIST=STACK","IO")
	HLIST.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_KAYNAKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_BAKIYEKAYNAK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_LATEST_B_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REZERVE_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REZ_BAKIYE","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "STOKLA_KARSILANAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_MAMULKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REVURETIMDENTESTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_STATUS","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "STOKTA_KALAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""

	Set MPSLIST = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MPS_LIST=STACK","IO")
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "R_MAMULKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "URT_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""

	Set MPSDetayList = Doc.Select2("MPS_DETAY_LST", "HMD_LIST", "", "", -1)
	Set MPSSubList = Doc.Select2("MPS_SUB_LST", "MPS_LIST", "", "", -1)

End Sub
Sub Hesapla()
	Call DetayListHesapla()
End Sub
Sub DetayListHesapla()
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")

	QStr = "SELECT MMPS10T.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, STOK00.AD KAYNAK_AD, STOK00.IUNIT IUNIT, MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK,"
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, S01.STOK_MIKTAR REZERVE_STOK, (MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK - COALESCE(S01.STOK_MIKTAR,0)) REZ_BAKIYE,"
	QStr = QStr & " (MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR - MMPS10E.SF_URETILEN) MAMUL_MIKTAR, S00.AD MAMUL_AD, MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT MAMUL_UNIT, "
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_STATUS, MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU, MMPS10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH,"
	QStr = QStr & " (CASE " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.BASLAMISMPS10='1' THEN 0 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='R' THEN 1 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='P' THEN 2 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='C' THEN 3 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='Q' THEN 4 " & _
								"ELSE 5 END) MPS_SIRA"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT ALLOC_MPSARTNO, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_MPSARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "' GROUP BY ALLOC_MPSARTNO) S01 ON MMPS10T.JOBNO=S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10E ON MMPS10E.EVRAKNO=MMPS10T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN BOMU01TM ON BOMU01TM.MAMULCODE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU AND BOMU01TM.AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMU01TM.VARSAYILAN10='1'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10T.ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND (BOMU01TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL OR STOK00.KART_TIPI='F')"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, MPS_SIRA, MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH"
	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfw3efsssd", QStr)


	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.KOD, Sum(STOK01.STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN GDEF00 ON GDEF00.KOD=STOK01.AMBCODE"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE GDEF00.MRPDEYOK10<>'1' AND ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK01.KOD"
	Set S01S = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdf34fdfd", QStr)

	Set HLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_LIST")
	Set HOZET = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_OZET")

	HLIST.Empty
	HOZET.Empty

	HLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	HOZET.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	EskiStokKodu = ""
	satirNo1 = 0
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & M10T.GetRowCount()
		if M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) <> EskiStokKodu Then
			Set S01S1 = Doc.Select2("sdf3wefsdf", S01S.GetAppName, "KOD='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S01S1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				StokMiktari = S01S1.STOK_MIKTAR(1)
			Else
				StokMiktari = 0
			End if
			KalanMiktar = StokMiktari
			satirNo1 = HOZET.AddRow
			HOZET.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			HOZET.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
			HOZET.IUNIT(satirNo1) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
			HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.SERBEST_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.ILK_IHTIYAC_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)

		satirNo = HLIST.AddRow
		HLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = M10T.EVRAKNO(i)
		HLIST.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		HLIST.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
		HLIST.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HLIST.IUNIT(satirNo) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
		HLIST.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		HLIST.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo) = M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HLIST.REZ_BAKIYE(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
		HLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_AD(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
		HLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = M10T.R_STATUS(i)
		HLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = M10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)

		if M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) > 0 and KalanMiktar > 0 Then
			if KalanMiktar < M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) Then
				Karsilanan = KalanMiktar
			Else
				Karsilanan = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
			End if
			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - Karsilanan
		Else
			Karsilanan = 0
		End if
		HLIST.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(satirNo) = Karsilanan
		HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) - Karsilanan
		HLIST.STOKTA_KALAN(satirNo) = KalanMiktar

		HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) + M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) + HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo)
		HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		if HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
			HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		Else
			HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
			if trim(HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1)) = "" Then
				HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next

	Call GetTumMPSList()

	HLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	HOZET.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub GetUretilebilenList()
	Set MDLIST = Doc.Select2("MPS_SUB_LST", "MPS_LIST", "URT_STATU='E'", "", -1)

Sub GetTumMPSList()
	Set HL = Doc.Select2("asdfd2wfwsdf", "HMD_LIST", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	Set MLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_LIST")
	MLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	MLIST.Empty

	For i = 1 to HL.GetRowCount()
		doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'lerin Üretilebilirlik Durumlarý Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & HL.GetRowCount()
		if HL.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiMPSNo Then
			satirNo = MLIST.AddRow
			MLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
			MLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = HL.R_MAMULKODU(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_AD(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = HL.R_STATUS(i)
			MLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = HL.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = 0
			MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "E"
		End if
		EskiMPSNo = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
		UretilebilecekMik = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(HL.REZERVE_STOK(i) + HL.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(i), HL.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)) * HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i), 3)
		if UretilebilecekMik < MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) Then
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = UretilebilecekMik
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) - UretilebilecekMik
			if MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "H"
			Else
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "K"
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Function GetGrupKoduQStr()
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")
	QStr = ""
	QStr = QStr & " STOK00.GK_1>='" & KRT.GK_1B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_1E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_1<='" & KRT.GK_1E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2>='" & KRT.GK_2B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_2E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2<='" & KRT.GK_2E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3>='" & KRT.GK_3B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_3E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3<='" & KRT.GK_3E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4>='" & KRT.GK_4B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_4E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4<='" & KRT.GK_4E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5>='" & KRT.GK_5B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_5E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5<='" & KRT.GK_5E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6>='" & KRT.GK_6B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_6E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6<='" & KRT.GK_6E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7>='" & KRT.GK_7B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_7E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7<='" & KRT.GK_7E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8>='" & KRT.GK_8B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_8E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8<='" & KRT.GK_8E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9>='" & KRT.GK_9B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_9E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9<='" & KRT.GK_9E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10>='" & KRT.GK_10B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_10E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10<='" & KRT.GK_10E & "'"
	End if
	GetGrupKoduQStr = QStr
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : _GNPRG99
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : MMPS_RHMD
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : Makro1
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 

FORM MMPS_RHMD SCROLLBARS=NO
SET CAPTION="MPS Hammadde Analizi"
SET AUTOFORMSIZE_RIGHTMARGIN=1

SET DEFAULT_FSTYLE=3

SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1

DEFINEFONT ID="default_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8
DEFINEFONT ID="default_text_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_edittext_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_button_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_checkbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG11
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kalýp Ýþ Emri Ekraný
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK=STACK","IO")
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "TOP_BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "OPERATOR","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "OPERATOR_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "BASLAYAN_OPERATOR","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "BASLAYAN_OPERATOR_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "TOP_METRAJI","DOUBLE",9,0,"R","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "BASLA","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "BITIR","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	MSTCK.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
'	Call ButonAktifPasif()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SRVP00", "")
End Sub

Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%OP%" Then
		MTable.OPERATOR = trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5))
		MTable.OPERATOR_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", MTable.OPERATOR, "")
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Call ButonAktifPasif()
		Exit Sub
	Elseif Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%TZ%" Then
		MTable.TEZGAH_KODU = trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5))
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Call ButonAktifPasif()
		Exit Sub
	Else
		Set SRV = Doc.Select1("axdfsg", "SRVP00", "KOD='" & MTable.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)
		if SRV.GetRowCount() = 0 Then 
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	MTable.MPSNO = SRV.KOD(1)
	MTable.KOD = SRV.KARTNO(1)
	MTable.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SRVPC0", "AD1", MTable.KOD, "")


	MTable.TOP_BARKOD = MTable.BARKOD

	Call ButonAktifPasif()
	MTable.BARKOD=""
End Sub

Sub cmdBasladi()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	if trim(MTable.BASLA) <> "E" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu aþamada 'Baþladý' butonuna basýlamaz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SRVP00", "")

	Set SRV0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00")
	Set SRVO = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00O")
	bos = ""
	Set SRVO1 = Doc.Select1("s0104h3", "SRVP00O", "KOD = '"& MTable.MPSNO &"' & RECTARIH1 <> '"& space(10) &"' & RECTARIH2 = '"& space(10) &"'", "", -1)

	if SRVO1.GetRecCount() = 0 then
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		SRV0.KOD = MTable.MPSNO

		satirNo = SRVO.AddRow
		SRVO.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = Doc.BuGun
		SRVO.RECTIME1(satirNo) = Doc.Saat(5)
		SRVO.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = ""
		SRVO.ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = MTable.TEZGAH_KODU	
		SRVO.OPERATORKODU(satirNo) = MTable.OPERATOR
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		MTable.Empty
	else

		Set SRVO2 = Doc.Select1("s000104h3", "SRVP00O", "OPERATORKODU = '"& MTable.OPERATOR &"'& RECTARIH1 <> '"& space(10) &"' & RECTARIH2 = '"& space(10) &"'", "", -1)
		if SRVO2.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
			for k = 1 to SRVO2.GetRecCount()
				doc.d7MsgBox "Bu personelin þuan " & trim(SRVO2.KOD(k))&" kodlu iþte açýlmýþ bir bildirimi var lütfen önce onu kapatýnýz...","Freedom",MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
				Call ButonAktifPasif()
			Next
		end if
		for i = 1 to SRVO1.GetRecCount()
			if SRVO1.RECTARIH1(i) <> Doc.BuGun() and trim(SRVO1.RECTARIH2(i)) = bos then
				doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Mesai gününden kalan açýk iþ olduðu için yeni üretim baþlangýcý yapamazsýnýz!" & vbcrlf &"Lütfen Kalýp sorumlunuzla görüþün...","Freedom",MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
				Call ButonAktifPasif()
			Elseif SRVO1.RECTARIH1(i) = Doc.BuGun() and trim(SRVO1.RECTARIH2(i)) = bos and SRVO1.OPERATORKODU(i) = MTable.OPERATOR then
				doc.d7MsgBox "Bu iþ için bugün açýlmýþ bir bildiriminiz var lütfen önce onu kapatýnýz...","Freedom",MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
				Call ButonAktifPasif()
			End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub cmdBitti()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	if trim(MTable.BITIR) <> "E" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu aþamada 'Bitti' butonuna basýlamaz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SRVP00", "")
	Set SRV0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00")
	Set SRVO = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00O")
	bos = ""
	Set SRVO1 = Doc.Select1("sdf34h3", "SRVP00O", "KOD = '"& MTable.MPSNO &"' & OPERATORKODU='" & MTable.OPERATOR & "' & ISMERKEZI = '"& MTable.TEZGAH_KODU &"' & RECTARIH1 <> '"& bos &"' & RECTARIH2 = '"& bos &"'", "", -1)

	if SRVO1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then

	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.MPSNO
		for i = 1 to SRVO.GetRowCount()
			if SRVO.RECTARIH1(i) <> Doc.BuGun() and trim(SRVO.RECTARIH2(i)) = bos then
				doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Mesai gününden kalan açýk iþ olduðu için yeni üretim baþlangýcý yapamazsýnýz!" & vbcrlf &"Lütfen Kalýp sorumlunuzla görüþün...","Freedom",MB_OK
				MTable.Empty
				Call ButonAktifPasif()
			Elseif SRVO.OPERATORKODU(i) = MTable.OPERATOR AND SRVO.ISMERKEZI(i) = MTable.TEZGAH_KODU AND SRVO.RECTARIH1(i) = DOC.BUGUN() AND trim(SRVO.RECTARIH2(i)) = bos then
				SRVO.SetCurrentRow(i)
				SRVO.RECTARIH2(i) = Doc.BuGun
				SRVO.RECTIME2(i) = Doc.Saat(5)
			End if
		Next
	End if
	
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	MTable.Empty
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	MTable.Empty
End Sub

Sub cmdVardiyaBitti()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	if trim(MTable.BITIR) <> "E" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu aþamada 'Vardiya Bitti' butonuna basýlamaz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
'	if trim(MTable.JOBNO) = "" Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma Yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
'		Exit Sub
'	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SRVP00", "")
	Set SRV0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00E")
	Set S32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00T")
	Set SRVO = Doc1.GetTableObject("SRVP00O")

	if MTable.TOP_METRAJI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Metraj Girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.EVRAKNO
	SRV0.RECTARIH2 = Doc.BuGun
	SRV0.RECTIME2 = Doc.Saat(5)
	Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "SRVP00E", "RECTIME2", 1

	Set SRVO1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf34h3", "SRVP00O", "OPERATOR='" & MTable.OPERATOR & "'", "", -1)
	if SRVO1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		satirNo = SRVO.AddRow
		SRVO.OPERATOR(satirNo) = MTable.OPERATOR
	End if

	S32T.SafePack
	if S32T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_MPS_MAMULLERI"
		if S32T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			S32T.SERINO(1) = trim(MTable.TOP_BARKOD)
		End if
	End if
'	S32T.SF_MIKTAR(1) = S32T.SF_MIKTAR(1) + MTable.TOP_METRAJI
'	SRV0.SF_MIKTAR = S32T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	MTable.Empty
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	MTable.Empty
End Sub

Sub ButonAktifPasif()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	if trim(MTable.TOP_BARKOD) = "" or trim(MTable.OPERATOR) = "" or trim(MTable.TEZGAH_KODU) = "" Then
		MTable.BASLA = "E"
		MTable.BITIR = "E"
	End if
End Sub
Sub Temizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_STACK")
	MTable.Empty
	Call ButonAktifPasif()
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable

	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

End Sub
Sub MamulKaliteOnayiVer()

	Set EvrakTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SatirTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEMG")

	if KontrolHataEMG(SatirTable) Then Exit Sub

	Depo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_MG
	OnayDepo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_MG_ONAY
	RedDepo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_MG_RED

	Call MGKaliteOnayiVer(EvrakTable, SatirTable, Depo, OnayDepo, RedDepo)
End Sub
Sub PKKaliteOnayiVer()
	Set EvrakTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SatirTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPK")
	Depo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_PK
	OnayDepo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_PK_ONAY
	RedDepo = EvrakTable.AMBCODE_PK_RED

	Call MGKaliteOnayiVer(EvrakTable, SatirTable, Depo, OnayDepo, RedDepo)
End Sub
Sub MGKaliteOnayiVer(EvrakTable, SatirTable, DepoKodu, OnayDepo, RedDepo)

	Set MGTable = SatirTable
	if KontrolHata(MGTable) Then Exit Sub

	Set MTable = EvrakTable
	Set TBL_ONAY = Doc.Select2("swdd_o", MGTable.GetAppName, "SELECTED='E'&ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if TBL_ONAY.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Call OnaylilariAktar(TBL_ONAY, OnayDepo)

	Set TBL_RED = Doc.Select2("swdd_oD", MGTable.GetAppName, "SELECTED='E'&RED_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if TBL_RED.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Call RedleriAktar(TBL_RED)

	Call UretimKaliteGoster(DepoKodu, SatirTable)
End Sub
Sub MetrajHatalilariMailGonder(KalanTable)
End Sub
Sub OnaylilariAktar(TBL_ONAY, HedefDepo)
	if TBL_ONAY.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set S26E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S26E.AMBCODE = TBL_ONAY.AMBCODE(1)
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE = HedefDepo

	For i = 1 to TBL_ONAY.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S26T.AddRow
		S26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S26T.KOD = TBL_ONAY.KOD(i)
		S26T.LOTNUMBER = TBL_ONAY.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S26T.SERINO = TBL_ONAY.SERINO(i)
		S26T.SF_MIKTAR = TBL_ONAY.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR(i)
		S26T.SF_SF_UNIT = TBL_ONAY.SF_UNIT(i)
		if trim(S26T.SF_SF_UNIT) <> trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S26T.KOD, "")) Then
			S26T.SF_AUTOCALC = "H"
			S26T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR = GFLSMik_Round(Doc.Div(TBL_ONAY.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR(i) * TBL_ONAY.STOK_MIKTAR1(i), TBL_ONAY.SF_MIKTAR(i)))
		End if
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = TBL_ONAY.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = TBL_ONAY.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = TBL_ONAY.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_C = TBL_ONAY.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = TBL_ONAY.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = TBL_ONAY.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S26T.LOCATION1 = TBL_ONAY.LOCATION1(i)
		S26T.LOCATION2 = TBL_ONAY.LOCATION2(i)
		S26T.LOCATION3 = TBL_ONAY.LOCATION3(i)
		S26T.LOCATION4 = TBL_ONAY.LOCATION4(i)
		S26T.FOKOD = TBL_ONAY.FOKOD(i)
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function GFLSMik_Round(Sayi)
	GFLS_Mik = Doc.Customp_Gets("GFLS_MIK")
	Nokta = InStr(1, GFLS_Mik, ".")
	Ondalik = Doc.CDbl(trim(Mid(GFLS_Mik, Nokta + 1)))
	GFLSMik_Round = Doc.Round(Sayi, Ondalik)
End Function
Sub MamulKaliteGoster()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SatirTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEMG")
	DepoKodu = MTable.AMBCODE_MG
	Call UretimKaliteGoster(DepoKodu, SatirTable)
End Sub
Sub PKKaliteGoster()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SatirTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPK")
	DepoKodu = MTable.AMBCODE_PK
	Call UretimKaliteGoster(DepoKodu, SatirTable)
End Sub
Sub UretimKaliteGoster(DepoKodu, SatirTable)
	Set MTable = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = SatirTable

	MTableT.Empty()

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("w3dede", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & DepoKodu & "'", "KOD+VRI_VR_R1+LOTNUMBER", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Alanlar = S01.GetFieldNames("")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Onaylanmamýþlar getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For each alan in Alanlar
			if Alan <> "STOK_MIKTAR" and InStr(1, Alan, "KOD_STOK00_") = 0 and InStr(1, Alan, "ALLOC_SIPARTNO_STOK40T_") = 0 _
					and InStr(1, Alan, "ALLOC_SIPNO_STOK40E_") = 0 Then
				alanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR1 = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
		MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT.RED_DEPOSU = MTable.AMBCODE_MG_RED
		MTableT.TASHIHMI = GetTashihStat(S01.KOD(i), S01.LOTNUMBER(i), S01.SERINO(i))
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Function KontrolHataEMG(Tablo)
	KontrolHataEMG = False
	Set S = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		if Tablo.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			if trim(Tablo.REF_TEXT_1(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr Hata Açýklamasý kolonu doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
				KontrolHataEMG = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Function KontrolHata(Tablo)
	KontrolHata = False
	Set S = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		if Tablo.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			if Tablo.RED_MIKTAR(i) > 0 And trim(Tablo.RED_DEPOSU(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr Red Deposu kolonu doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
				KontrolHata = True
			End if
			if trim(Tablo.NOTES2(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr Hata Açýklamasý kolonu doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
				KontrolHata = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

'* GÝRÝÞ KALÝTE ***************
Sub SIKaliteOnayiVer()
	Set SITable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESI")
	if KontrolHata(SITable) Then Exit Sub

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Set TBL_ONAY = Doc.Select2("swdd_o", "MAKROTABLESI", "SELECTED='E'&ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	Call OnaylilariAktar(TBL_ONAY, MTable.AMBCODE_SI_ONAY)

	Set TBL_RED = Doc.Select2("swdd_oD", "MAKROTABLESI", "SELECTED='E'&RED_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if TBL_RED.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Call RedleriAktar(TBL_RED)

	Call SIKaliteGoster()
End Sub
Sub RedleriAktar(TBL_RED)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set S26E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Doc1.New_Voucher()

	S26E.AMBCODE = TBL_RED.AMBCODE(1)
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE = TBL_RED.RED_DEPOSU(1)

	For i = 1 to TBL_RED.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S26T.AddRow
		S26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S26T.KOD = TBL_RED.KOD(i)
		S26T.LOTNUMBER = TBL_RED.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S26T.SERINO = TBL_RED.SERINO(i)
		S26T.SF_MIKTAR = TBL_RED.RED_MIKTAR(i)
		S26T.SF_SF_UNIT = TBL_RED.SF_UNIT(i)
		S26T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = TBL_RED.RED_DEPOSU(i)
		if trim(S26T.SF_SF_UNIT) <> trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S26T.KOD, "")) Then
			S26T.SF_AUTOCALC = "H"
			S26T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR = TBL_RED.STOK_MIKTAR1(i)
		End if
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = TBL_RED.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = TBL_RED.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = TBL_RED.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_VR_C = TBL_RED.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = TBL_RED.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S26T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = TBL_RED.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S26T.LOCATION1 = TBL_RED.LOCATION1(i)
		S26T.LOCATION2 = TBL_RED.LOCATION2(i)
		S26T.LOCATION3 = TBL_RED.LOCATION3(i)
		S26T.LOCATION4 = TBL_RED.LOCATION4(i)
		S26T.FOKOD = TBL_RED.FOKOD(i)
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub SIKaliteGoster()
	Set MTable = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLESI")
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("w3dede", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE_SI & "'", "KOD+LOTNUMBER", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	MTableT.Empty()

	Alanlar = S01.GetFieldNames("")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		IrsaliyeNo = ""
		TedarikciIrsNo = ""
		TedarikciCHKodu = ""
		IrsaliyeTarihi = ""
		EvrakTuru = ""

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Onaylanmamýþlar getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S01.LOTNUMBER(i)) <> "" Then
			QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
			if trim(MTable.CARIHESAPCODE) <> "" Then
				QStr = QStr & "&CARIHESAPCODE='" & MTable.CARIHESAPCODE & "'"
			End if
			Set S29T = Doc.Select1("dde33", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
			if S29T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				IrsaliyeNo = S29T.EVRAKNO
				TedarikciIrsNo = S29T.XIRSALIYENO
				TedarikciCHKodu = S29T.CARIHESAPCODE
				IrsaliyeTarihi = S29T.TARIH
				EvrakTuru = "SI"
			Else
				QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
				Set S68T = Doc.Select1("dde33", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
				if trim(MTable.CARIHESAPCODE) <> "" Then
					QStr = QStr & "&CHCODE='" & MTable.CARIHESAPCODE & "'"
				End if
				if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
					IrsaliyeNo = S68T.EVRAKNO
					TedarikciIrsNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK68E", "XIRSALIYENO", S68T.EVRAKNO, "")
					TedarikciCHKodu = S68T.CHCODE
					IrsaliyeTarihi = S68T.TARIH
					EvrakTuru = "FG"
				End if
			End if
		End if
		if (trim(TedarikciIrsNo) = trim(MTable.XIRSALIYENO) or trim(MTable.XIRSALIYENO) = "") and _
			(trim(TedarikciCHKodu) = trim(MTable.CARIHESAPCODE) or trim(MTable.CARIHESAPCODE) = "") Then
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For each alan in Alanlar
				alanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				if Alan <> "STOK_MIKTAR" Then
					MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
				End if
			Next
			MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR1 = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
			MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			MTableT.RED_DEPOSU = MTable.AMBCODE_SI_RED
			MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = EvrakTuru
			MTableT.IRSALIYENO = IrsaliyeNo
			MTableT.TARIH = Doc.StrToNorDate(IrsaliyeTarihi)
			MTableT.XIRSALIYENO = TedarikciIrsNo
			MTableT.CARIHESAPCODE = TedarikciCHKodu
			MTableT.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", TedarikciCHKodu, "")
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.Sort("TARIH+EVRAKTYPE+IRSALIYENO")
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub MG_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEMG")
	if MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR > MTableT.SF_MIKTAR Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Onaylanan Miktar Ýþlem Miktarýndan Büyük Olamaz", "Hata", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
		MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
Sub PK_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_OV()
	Set MTablePK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPK")
	if MTablePK.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR > MTablePK.SF_MIKTAR Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Onaylanan Miktar Ýþlem Miktarýndan Büyük Olamaz", "Hata", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
		MTablePK.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR = MTablePK.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
Sub SI_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESI")
	if MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR > MTableT.SF_MIKTAR Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Onaylanan Miktar Ýþlem Miktarýndan Büyük Olamaz", "Hata", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
		MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Sub MG_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEMG")
	MTableT.RED_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR - MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub PK_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTablePK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPK")
	MTablePK.RED_MIKTAR = MTablePK.SF_MIKTAR - MTablePK.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SI_ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESI")
	MTableT.RED_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR - MTableT.ONAYLANAN_MIKTAR
End Sub

	MTable.Addfield2 "FF_ARTTIRIM_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",18,2,"R","",""

	MTable.Addfield2 "FF_AZALTMA_ET", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "FF_AZALTMA_EN", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "FF_AZALTMA_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",18,2,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "FF_AZALTMA_MH", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "S18_EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TR_GK_1", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""

End Sub
Function GetTashihStat(StokKodu, LotNo, SeriNo)
	GetTashihStat = ""
	MPSNo = ""
	if trim(SeriNo) <> "" Then
		MPSNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "MPSNO", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SeriNo), 30), "")
	Else
		Set S20T = Doc.Select1G("ef23fsdf", "STOK20T", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & LotNo & "'", "", "MPSNO", -1)
		if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
			MPSNo = S20T.MPSNO
		End if
	End if
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" Then Exit Function

	Plan = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "HAVUZKODU", MPSNo, "")
	if trim(Plan) = "T" Then
		GetTashihStat = "E"
	End if
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : AKTAR2
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Surtel Ambalaj 'a Aktarma Makrosu (Silmeyin!!!!!)
Kod : 
Public HedefFirma
Public Const SurtelKabloCHKodu = "4S003"  'Surtel Ambalaj 'daki Surtel Kablo C/H Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Call IrsaliyeAktar()
End Sub

Sub FaturaAktar()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK70E) Then Set STOK70E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK70T) Then Set STOK70T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")

	Call SetHedefFirma()

	if STOK70E.TARIH < "2011/04/01" Then Exit Sub

	if trim(STOK70E.CH_KODU) <> "1S082" and trim(STOK70E.CH_KODU) <> "4S008" Then Exit Sub

	STOK70T.SafePack()
	if STOK70T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK74", "", HedefFirma)
	if Doc1 is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda satýnalma faturasý evraký yaratýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S74E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK74E")
	Set S74T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK74T")

	if KodEksik2(Doc1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK70E.EVRAKNO
	S74E.CH_KODU = SurtelKabloCHKodu
	S74E.TARIH = STOK70E.TARIH

  if TRIM(STOK70E.NITELIK)="XK" then
   	S74E.NITELIK = "Z1"
  else
    	S74E.NITELIK = STOK70E.NITELIK
  end if

	S74E.CH_KUR = STOK70E.CH_KUR
	S74E.CH_KURV = STOK70E.CH_KURV
	
	S74T.Empty
	For i = 1 to STOK70T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S74T.AddRow()
		S74T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S74T.KOD(satirNo) = STOK70T.KOD(i)
		S74T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = STOK70T.TRNUM(i)
		S74T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S74T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = STOK70T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S74T.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = STOK70T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i)
		S74T.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = Doc.DefaultPB
     if  TRIM(STOK70E.NITELIK)<>"XK" then
   		S74T.KDV_ORANI(satirNo) =STOK70T.KDV_ORANI(i)
     end if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Fatura " & HedefFirma & " Firmasýna Aktarýldý", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
End Sub
Function KodEksik2(Doc1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	KodEksik2 = False
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2G("df234gsdh", "STOK70T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		Set S00 = Doc1.Select1("fgerhgdeh", "STOK00", "KOD='" & S70T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if StokKoduAktar(S70T.KOD(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(S70T.KOD(i)) & " stok kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK		
				KodEksik2 = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub IrsaliyeAktar()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60E) Then Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60T) Then Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")

	if STOK60E.TARIH < "2011/04/01" Then Exit Sub

	Call SetHedefFirma()

	if trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "1S082" and trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "4S008" Then Exit Sub

	if trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye Sýra No alaný doldurulmamýþ. Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlmadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK60T.SafePack()
	if STOK60T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	if STOK60T.SIPNO(1) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ baðlantýsý yapýlmamýþ. Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlmadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	SipCHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", STOK60T.SIPNO(1), "")

'	SurtelKabloCHKodu = "4S003"

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK60", "", HedefFirma)
	if Doc1 is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda irsaliye evraký yaratýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set S60E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60T")

	if KodEksik(Doc1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " firmasýna aktarýlamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if


	Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO
	S60E.CH_KODU = SipCHKodu
	S60E.AMBCODE = STOK60E.AMBCODE
	S60E.TARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
	
	S60T.Empty
	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S60T.AddRow()
		S60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S60T.KOD(satirNo) = STOK60T.KOD(i)
		S60T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = STOK60T.TRNUM(i)
		S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S60T.NOTES(satirNo) = STOK60T.SERINO(i)

'		S60T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK60T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
'		Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T", "SF_MIKTAR", satirNo
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye " & HedefFirma & " Firmasýna Aktarýldý", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
End Sub
Function KodEksik(Doc1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	KodEksik = False
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("df234gsdh", "STOK60T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		Set S00 = Doc1.Select1("fgerhgdeh", "STOK00", "KOD='" & S60T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			if StokKoduAktar(S60T.KOD(i)) <> 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox HedefFirma & " firmasýnda " & trim(S60T.KOD(i)) & " stok kodu yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK		
				KodEksik = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function StokKoduAktar(StokKodu)
	Call SetHedefFirma()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK00", "", HedefFirma)
	Set S00_1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00_2 = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK00")	

	Doc2.Load_Voucher TRIM(StokKodu)
	Doc1.Load_Voucher TRIM(StokKodu)
	S00_1.AD = S00_2.AD
	S00_1.IUNIT = S00_2.IUNIT
	S00_1.CODETYPE = "D"
	S00_1.GK_9 = "1"
  

	StokKoduAktar = Doc1.Save_Voucher

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function SurtelAmbalajIrsaliyeFaturalanmis()
	SurtelAmbalajIrsaliyeFaturalanmis = False

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60E) Then Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK60T) Then Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")

	if trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "1S082" and trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU) <> "4S008" Then Exit Function

	Call SetHedefFirma()


	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK60", "", HedefFirma)

	IrsaliyeNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO), 10)
	Set S60T = Doc1.Select1("sdf34fwerg", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='" & IrsaliyeNo & "'&SF_FATURALANANMIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if S60T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		SurtelAmbalajIrsaliyeFaturalanmis = True
	End if
End Function
Sub SetHedefFirma()
	HedefFirma = Replace(trim(Doc.Getd7FirmCode), "SURTEL", "AMBALAJ")
End Sub

Makro Kodu : CAVO2
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
