Açýklama : AÇIK MPS LERÝN TEZGAH BAZINDA SÜRELERÝ
Kod : 
Public fark,Sanal_M10T
Sub Makro1() 
	 
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Sh1.Cells(2,2).Value = "AÇIK MPS LERÝN TEZGAH BAZINDA SÜRELERÝ"
	Sh1.Cells(3,2).Value = "BÖLÜM ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,3).Value = "HÜCRE ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,4).Value = "TEZGAH KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,5).Value = "TEZGAH ADI"

	Son_Tarih = Inputbox("Raporlamak Ýstediðiniz Son Tarih" & vbcrlf &"GG/AA/YYYY Formatýnda giriniz.","Freedom")

	Excel.Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 5
	Excel.Columns(2).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(3).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(5).ColumnWidth = 50

	satir = 4
	Set mpstarihi=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	Set bugun=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	Set Son_Gun=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	bos = ""
	mpsno = ""

	Son_Gun.SetDate(Son_Tarih)
	Son_hafta = Son_Gun.GetWOY_ISO()

'	bugun.SetDate("01/01/2012")
	bugun.SetDate(doc.bugun)
	buhafta = bugun.GetWOY_ISO()

	if son_hafta = 0 then
		mesaj = "Girmiþ olduðunuz tarih algýlanamadý!..." & vbcrlf & "Lütfen tarihi GG/AA/YYYY formatýnda belirtiniz."
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(mesaj,"Freedom",MB_ICONSTOP)
		Exit sub
	End if

	fark = son_hafta - buhafta
	if fark < 0 then
		mesaj = "Ýçinden bulunduðumuz hafta" & buhafta & ". haftadýr Lütfen raporlamak için daha büyük bir hafta giriniz."
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(mesaj,"Freedom",MB_ICONSTOP)
		Exit sub
	End if

	fark = fark + 1

		Sh1.Cells(3,6).Value = "GECÝKEN"&k
	for k = 1 to fark
		Sh1.Cells(3,6+k).Value = "HAFTA_"&k
	Next

'********************************************
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(3,1),Sh1.Cells(3,6+fark)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,6+fark)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge

	Sh1.Columns("F:BZ").NumberFormat = "#,##0.00" '//  noktadan sonra 

'********************************************

	Call CreateTable(fark)

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("Ssda23","MMPS10T","ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"' & R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)

	if M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for ii = 1 to M10T.GetRecCount()

			satirNo = Sanal_M10T.AddRow()
			Sanal_M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			mpstarihi.SetDate(M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(ii))
			hafta = mpstarihi.GetWOY_ISO()

			opr_birimsure = M10T.R_MIKTART(ii)
			tezgahkodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(ii)
			tezgahadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgk1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_1",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgk2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_2",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahadam = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","B_ISCILIKSAATI",tezgahkodu,"")
			adam_sure = opr_birimsure * tezgahadam

			Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD1 = tezgahgk1
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD2 = tezgahgk1
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD = tezgahkodu
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_AD = tezgahadi

			kacincihafta = hafta - buhafta
			if kacincihafta <= 0 then
				if kacincihafta < 0 then
					Sanal_M10T.GEC = adam_sure
				End if 
				kacincihafta = 1
			Else
				kacincihafta = kacincihafta + 1
			End if

			zaman = "HAFTA_" & kacincihafta
			Sanal_M10T.SetFieldValue zaman,satirNo,adam_sure
		next
	End if

	Sanal_M10T.GROUPBY("TZ_KOD")

'********************************************
'	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount()+2,6+fark)).Select


'		Excel.Selection.Borders(1).ThemeColor = 2
'		Excel.Selection.Borders(4).ThemeColor = 2
'		Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 1


'		Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
'		Excel.Selection.LineStyle = xlContinuous
'		Excel.Selection.ColorIndex = 0
'		Excel.Selection.TintAndShade = 0
'		Excel.Selection.Weight = xlThin

'********************************************

	if Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount()

			Sh1.Cells(satir,2).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD1(kk)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,3).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD2(kk)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,4).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD(kk)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,5).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_AD(kk)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,6).Value = Sanal_M10T.GEC(kk)

			for k = 1 to fark
				Excel.Columns(5+k+1).ColumnWidth = 10

				hafta = "HAFTA_"&k
'				ssure = Sanal_M10T.GetFieldValue (hafta,kk)
				Sh1.Cells(satir,6+k).Value = Sanal_M10T.GetFieldValue (hafta,kk)

			Next

			satir = satir + 1
		next
	End if

	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Sub CreateTable(fark)
	Set Sanal_M10T = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_GK_KOD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_GK_KOD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "GEC","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	for zz = 1 to fark
		hafta = "HAFTA_"&zz
		Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 hafta,"DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Next

	Sanal_M10T.Empty()
	Sanal_M10T.AddReportfields
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ALK_2012 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CAVO3
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : CAVO - Excel ile Müþteri Öngörüsünden Rapor.
Kod : 
Public tarih_al, May_container
Sub Makro1() 
	 
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Call CreateTable()
	Call	Satis_Ongoruleri()
	Call Musteri_Ongoruleri()
	Call Kesin_Siparis()
	Call Onceki_Yil_Gerceklesen()
	Call Bu_Yil_Gerceklesen()

	MyArray = Array("Ocak", "Subat", "Mart", "Nisan", "Mayý", "Haziran", "Temmuz", "Agustos", "Eylul", "Ekim", "Kasým", "Aralýk") 
	
	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = "Kod"
	Sh1.Cells(1,2).Value = "Stok Adý"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Value = "Grup Kodu 8"

	For r = 1 To UBound(MyArray) + 1
	yer_atla = 0
	if r > 1 Then yer_atla = 5
		Sh1.Cells(1,7 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Sat.Ön. "& MyArray(r-1)
		Sh1.Cells(1,8 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Müþ.Ön. "&MyArray(r-1)
		Sh1.Cells(1,9 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Kes.Sip "&MyArray(r-1)	
		Sh1.Cells(1,10 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Geç.Fat "&MyArray(r-1)	
		Sh1.Cells(1,11 + yer_atla * (r -1)).Value = "Bu.Fat "&MyArray(r-1)
	Next
	For t = 1 To 12
		Sh1.Columns(7).Interior.Color = RGB(223,255,255)
		if t > 1 Then
			Sh1.Columns(7 + (t - 1) * 5).Interior.Color = RGB(223,255,255)
		End if
	Next

'Sh1.Columns("A:F").Select
'Sh1.Range("A:F").Select
'Sh1.Range("A:C").Interior.Color = RGB(127,187,199)
'Sh1.Columns(1).EntireColumn.Hidden = True

	Sh1.Columns("A:C").ColumnWidth = 25
'	Sh1.Columns("C:F").ColumnWidth = 0
	Sh1.Columns("G:BZ").ColumnWidth = 15
	Sh1.Rows("1:1").Interior.Color = RGB(202,225,255)
	'Sh1.Columns("G:BZ").NumberFormat = "#,##0.00" '//  noktadan sonra 2 hane
	Sh1.Columns("G:BZ").NumberFormat = "#,##0" '//  noktadan sonra 0 hane

	May_container.Sort("kod")
	May_container.GROUPBY("KOD")

	Dim ad_kod, GK_1
	For j = 1 To May_container.GetRecCount()
		ad_kod = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", May_container.KOD(j), "")
		May_container.AD(j) = ad_kod
		ad_kod = "" 
		GK_1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_8", May_container.KOD(j), "")
		May_container.GK_1(j) = GK_1
		GK_1 = "" 
	Next

	For k = 1 To May_container.GetRecCount()
		Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 1)  = May_container.KOD(k)
		Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 2)  = May_container.AD(k)
		Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 3)  = May_container.GK_1(k)
		Sayi = Doc.Fmod(k,2)
		if Sayi = 0 Then Sh1.Rows(k).Interior.Color = RGB(223,255,255)
		For r = 1 To 12
			if r =  1  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_OCAK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 7) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_OCAK(k)
			if r =  2  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_SUBAT(k) <> 0 Then  Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 12) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_SUBAT(k)
			if r =  3  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MART(k) <> 0 Then  Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 17) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MART(k)
			if r =  4  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_NISAN(k) <> 0 Then  Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 22) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_NISAN(k)
			if r =  5  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MAYIS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 27) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MAYIS(k)
			if r =  6  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_HAZIRAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 32) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_HAZIRAN(k)
			if r =  7  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_TEMMUZ(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 37) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_TEMMUZ(k)
			if r =  8  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_AGUSTOS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 42) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_AGUSTOS(k)
			if r =  9  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EYLUL(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 47) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EYLUL(k)
			if r = 10  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EKIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 52) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EKIM(k)
			if r = 11  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_KASIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 57) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_KASIM(k)
			if r = 12  And May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_ARALIK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 52) = May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_ARALIK(k)

			if r =  1 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_OCAK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 8) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_OCAK(k) 
			if r =  2 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_SUBAT(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 13) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_SUBAT(k) 
			if r =  3 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_MART(k)<> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 18) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_MART(k)
			if r =  4 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_NISAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 23) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_NISAN(k)  
			if r =  5 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_MAYIS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 28) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_MAYIS(k)  
			if r =  6 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_HAZIRAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 33) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_HAZIRAN(k)  
			if r =  7 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_TEMMUZ(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 38) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_TEMMUZ(k)  
			if r =  8 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_AGUSTOS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 43) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_AGUSTOS(k)  
			if r =  9 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_EYLUL(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 48) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_EYLUL(k)  
			if r = 10 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_EKIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 53) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_EKIM(k)  
			if r = 11 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_KASIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 58) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_KASIM(k)  
			if r = 12 And May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_ARALIK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 63) = May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_ARALIK(k)  

			if r =  1 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_OCAK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 9) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_OCAK(k)
			if r =  2 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_SUBAT(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 14) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_SUBAT(k)
			if r =  3 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_MART(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 19) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_MART(k)
			if r =  4 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_NISAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 24) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_NISAN(k)
			if r =  5 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_MAYIS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 29) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_MAYIS(k)
			if r =  6 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_HAZIRAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 34) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_HAZIRAN(k)
			if r =  7 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_TEMMUZ(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 39) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_TEMMUZ(k)
			if r =  8 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_AGUSTOS(k)<> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 44) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_AGUSTOS(k)
			if r =  9 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_EYLUL(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 49) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_EYLUL(k)
			if r = 10 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_EKIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 54) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_EKIM(k)
			if r = 11 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_KASIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 59) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_KASIM(k)
			if r = 12 And May_container.KESIN_SIP_ARALIK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 64) = May_container.KESIN_SIP_ARALIK(k)

			if r =  1 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_OCAK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 10) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_OCAK(k)
			if r =  2 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_SUBAT(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 15) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_SUBAT(k)
			if r =  3 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MART(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 20) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MART(k)
			if r =  4 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_NISAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 25) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_NISAN(k)
			if r =  5 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MAYIS(k)<> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 30) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MAYIS(k)
			if r =  6 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_HAZIRAN(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 35) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_HAZIRAN(k)
			if r =  7 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_TEMMUZ(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 40) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_TEMMUZ(k)
			if r =  8 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_AGUSTOS(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 45) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_AGUSTOS(k)
			if r =  9 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EYLUL(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 50) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EYLUL(k)
			if r = 10 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EKIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 55) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EKIM(k)
			if r = 11 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_KASIM(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 60) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_KASIM(k)
			if r = 12 And May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_ARALIK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 65) = May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_ARALIK(k)

			if r =  1 And May_container.BU_YIL_FATURA_OCAK(k) <> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 11) = May_container.BU_YIL_FATURA_OCAK(k)

			if r =  3 And May_container.BU_YIL_FATURA_MART(k)<> 0 Then Sh1.Cells(k + 1, 21) = May_container.BU_YIL_FATURA_MART(k)

			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel Hazýrlanýyor...  Lütfen Bekleyin."

	Next

End Sub

Sub Satis_Ongoruleri()
Dim OT_1L_2, AY, OTOSP_1E
	Set OT_1L_2 = Doc.Select1("ot_han_2", "OTOSP1L", "ACIKKAPALI ='A'  &  ACIKKAPALI2 ='A'  & GK_1 = 'BUTCE12'", "", -1) '// cari ve grup kodu 1 e göre
	Set OTOSP_1E = Doc.Select1("DASD3432OLK", "OTOSP1E", "ACIKKAPALI ='A'", "", -1) 
  tarih_al = Doc.StrDate_YEAR(OTOSP_1E.TARIH(1))

	For i = 1 To OT_1L_2.GetRecCount()
		satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
		May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		May_container.KOD = OT_1L_2.KOD(i) 
		AY = Doc.StrDate_Month(OT_1L_2.RTESTARIH(i))
		if AY =  1 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_OCAK = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  2 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_SUBAT = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  3 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MART = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  4 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_NISAN = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  5 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_MAYIS = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  6 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_HAZIRAN = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  7 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_TEMMUZ = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  8 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_AGUSTOS = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  9 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EYLUL = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 10 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_EKIM = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 11 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_KASIM = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 12 Then May_container.SATIS_ONGORU_ARALIK = OT_1L_2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Satis Öngoruleri Hazýrlanýyor...  Lütfen Bekleyin."			
	Next
End Sub

		if AY =  6 Then May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_HAZIRAN = OTOSP_1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  7 Then May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_TEMMUZ = OTOSP_1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  8 Then May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_AGUSTOS = OTOSP_1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		if AY = 12 Then May_container.MUSTERI_ONGORU_ARALIK = OTOSP_1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)

Sub Kesin_Siparis()
Dim	STOK40_T, ta_rih, AY
	ta_rih = tarih_al&"/01/01"
	Qstr = " SELECT * FROM STOK40T "
	Qstr = Qstr &	" WHERE  TARIH >='"&ta_rih&"' AND "
	Qstr = Qstr &	" KOD IN (SELECT KOD FROM OTOSP1L WHERE ACIKKAPALI='A' AND ACIKKAPALI2='A') "	
	Set STOK40_T= Doc.RunSQLQuery("KKK34d94", Qstr) 

	For i = 1 To STOK40_T.GetRecCount()
		satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
		May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		May_container.KOD = STOK40_T.KOD(i) 
		AY = Doc.StrDate_Month(STOK40_T.RTESTARIH(i))
		if AY =  1 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_OCAK = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  2 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_SUBAT = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  3 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_MART = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  4 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_NISAN = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  5 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_MAYIS = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  6 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_HAZIRAN = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  7 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_TEMMUZ = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  8 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_AGUSTOS = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  9 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_EYLUL = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 10 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_EKIM = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 11 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_KASIM = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 12 Then May_container.KESIN_SIP_ARALIK = STOK40_T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kesin Sipariþ Bilgileri Hazýrlanýyor...  Lütfen Bekleyin."
	Next
	
End Sub

Sub Onceki_Yil_Gerceklesen()
Dim	STOK7X_T	, tarih_tut, ta_rih, ta_rih_2, AY, Qstr
	tarih_tut = tarih_al - 1
	ta_rih = tarih_tut&"/01/01"
	ta_rih_2 = tarih_tut&"/31/12"
	
	Qstr = " SELECT * FROM STOK7XT "
	Qstr = Qstr &	" WHERE  TARIH >='"&ta_rih&"' AND TARIH <'"&ta_rih_2&"' AND "
	Qstr = Qstr &	" KOD IN (SELECT KOD FROM OTOSP1L WHERE ACIKKAPALI='A' AND ACIKKAPALI2='A') "
	Set STOK7X_T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("23draesgfd978", Qstr) 
	For i = 1 To STOK7X_T.GetRecCount()
		satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
		May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		May_container.KOD = STOK7X_T.KOD(i)
		AY = Doc.StrDate_Month(STOK7X_T.TARIH(i))
		if AY =  1 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_OCAK = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  2 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_SUBAT = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  3 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MART = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  4 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_NISAN = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  5 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MAYIS = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  6 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_HAZIRAN = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  7 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_TEMMUZ = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  8 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_AGUSTOS = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY =  9 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EYLUL = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 10 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_EKIM = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 11 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_KASIM = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		if AY = 12 Then May_container.GECEN_YIL_FATURA_ARALIK = STOK7X_T.SINFO_MIKTAR(i)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Önceki Yil Gercekleþen Faturalar Hazýrlanýyor...  Lütfen Bekleyin."
	Next
End Sub

Sub CreateTable()
	Set May_container = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	May_container.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "GK_1","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""

	May_container.Addfield2 "SATIS_ONGORU_OCAK","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_ONGORU_OCAK","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "KESIN_SIP_OCAK","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "BU_YIL_FATURA_OCAK","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	May_container.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_ONGORU_SUBAT","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "KESIN_SIP_SUBAT","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "BU_YIL_FATURA_SUBAT","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "GECEN_YIL_FATURA_SUBAT","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	May_container.Addfield2 "SATIS_ONGORU_MART","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_ONGORU_MART","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "KESIN_SIP_MART","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "BU_YIL_FATURA_MART","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "GECEN_YIL_FATURA_MART","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	May_container.Addfield2 "SATIS_ONGORU_NISAN","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	May_container.Addfield2 "KESIN_SIP_HAZIRAN","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "BU_YIL_FATURA_HAZIRAN","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	May_container.Addfield2 "GECEN_YIL_FATURA_HAZIRAN","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

Public Sanal_M10T
Sub Makro1() 
	
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.WorkBooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)

	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = "AÇIK MPS'LERÝN TEZGAH BAZINDA HAFTALIK MIKTARLARI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,1).Value = "TEZGAH GRUP KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,2).Value = "TEZGAH ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,3).Value = "STOK KODU"
	Sh1.Cells(3,4).Value = "STOK ADI"
	Sh1.Cells(3,5).Value = "HAFTA1"
	Sh1.Cells(3,6).Value = "HAFTA2"
	Sh1.Cells(3,7).Value = "HAFTA3"
	Sh1.Cells(3,8).Value = "HAFTA4"
	Sh1.Cells(3,9).Value = "HAFTA5"
	Sh1.Cells(3,10).Value = "HAFTA6"
	Sh1.Cells(3,11).Value = "ÝÞ EMÝRÝ"

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(3,1),Sh1.Cells(3,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,11)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge

	Excel.Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 19
	Excel.Columns(2).ColumnWidth = 20
	Excel.Columns(3).ColumnWidth = 22
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 50
	Excel.Columns(5).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(6).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(7).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(8).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(9).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(10).ColumnWidth = 10
	Excel.Columns(11).ColumnWidth = 20

	HAFTA1 = 0
	HAFTA2 = 0
	HAFTA3 = 0
	HAFTA4 = 0
	HAFTA5 = 0
	HAFTA6 = 0

	satir = 4
	Set mpstarihi=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	Set bugun=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	bos = ""
	mpsno = ""
	mpsmiktari = 0
	satirNo = 1

	Call CreateTable()

'	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("Ssda23","MMPS10T","ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"' & R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I' & R_LATEST_B_TARIH > '17/01/2012'","",-1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("Ssda23","MMPS10T","ACIK_KAPALI = '"& bos &"' & R_KAYNAKTYPE = 'I'","",-1)

	if M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to M10T.GetRecCount()

'		if kk = 100 then 
'			exit for
'		End if

			satirNo = Sanal_M10T.AddRow()
			Sanal_M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

'			bugun.SetDate("25/01/2012")
			bugun.SetDate(doc.bugun)
			buhafta = bugun.GetWOY_ISO()

			mpstarihi.SetDate(M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(kk))
			hafta = mpstarihi.GetWOY_ISO()
			mpsmiktari = M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(kk)
			mpsno = M10T.EVRAKNO(kk)

			tezgahkodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(kk)
			tezgahadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgk1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","GK_1",tezgahkodu,"")
			tezgahgkadi = Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK1","AD",tezgahgk1,-1)

			stokkodu = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(kk)
			stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")

			fark = hafta - buhafta
			if fark <= 0 then
				fark = 0
			End if
			
			Select Case fark
			Case "0"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_1 = mpsmiktari
				Sanal_M10T.MPS_NO = mpsno
			Case "1"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_2 = mpsmiktari
			Case "2"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_3 = mpsmiktari
			Case "3"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_4 = mpsmiktari
			Case "4"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_5 = mpsmiktari
			Case "5"
				Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_6 = mpsmiktari
			End Select
		
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD = trim(tezgahgkadi)
			Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD = trim(tezgahadi)
			Sanal_M10T.KOD = trim(stokkodu)
			Sanal_M10T.AD = trim(stokadi)
		Next
	End if

'	Sanal_M10T.Sort("HAFTA_1")

'	Sanal_M10T.GROUPBY("TZ_KOD+KOD+MPS_NO")  '////tercih
	Sanal_M10T.GROUPBY("TZ_KOD+KOD")

	if Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for ii = 1 to Sanal_M10T.GetRecCount()

			Sh1.Cells(satir,1).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_GK_KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,2).Value = Sanal_M10T.TZ_KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,3).Value = Sanal_M10T.KOD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,4).Value = Sanal_M10T.AD(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,5).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_1(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,6).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_2(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,7).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_3(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,8).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_4(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,9).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_5(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,10).Value = Sanal_M10T.HAFTA_6(ii)
			Sh1.Cells(satir,11).Value = Sanal_M10T.MPS_NO(ii)

			satir = satir + 1
		next
	End if
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Sub CreateTable()
	Set Sanal_M10T = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_GK_KOD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "TZ_KOD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_1","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_2","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_3","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_4","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_5","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "HAFTA_6","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Addfield2 "MPS_NO","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Sanal_M10T.Empty()
	Sanal_M10T.AddReportfields
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Public SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Yazildi, PrintMode, tmpFolder, chkodu,DT5,satirSayisi,satiryuksekligi,baslik_baslangic,Ondalik
Public Grid()
Public satirBaslangic,dip_tutar

Const cdoSendUsingPickup = 1 
Const cdoSendUsingPort = 2 
Const cdoAnonymous = 0
Const cdoBasic = 1
Const cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM

Sub Makro1()
	chkodu = "120 20 VAL23"
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("CARI00TV","")
	nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(chkodu)
	Doc1.RunCommand"CMD_LPB_YASLANDIRMA_LISTELE"

	Set DT5 = Doc1.GetTableObject("DT05")
	satirSayisi = DT5.GetRecCount()

	if satirSayisi > 0 then 
		Call Form()
	End if

End Sub

sub Form()

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	SayfaEni = 210
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Mutabakat Mektubu",SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni,"")
	Yazildi	 = 0
	PrintMode = 1


	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = 10
	UstMargin = 5 
	AltMargin = 5
	SatirYuksekligi = 5
	BaslikGenislik = 45
	BilgiGenislik  = 100

	baslik_baslangic = 50
	satirBaslangic = baslik_baslangic + SatirYuksekligi * 13


	DisKutuGenislik = SayfaEni - (SolMargin + SagMargin)
	DisKutuBoyu = SayfaBoyu - (UstMargin + AltMargin)

	Res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Res = Pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	chadi=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,"")
	adres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",chkodu,"")
	adres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",chkodu,"")
	adres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",chkodu,"")
	telefon = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",chkodu,"")
	fax = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",chkodu,"")
	eposta = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","EMAIL",chkodu,"")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin, SolMargin, 18,30,logo,114)

	imza = "Mebant Ýzalasyon ve Bant San. A.Þ."
	Mesaj = "Sayýn Müþterimiz," & vbcrlf & "Mevcut vadesi geçmiþ borç bakiyeniz aþaðýda belirtilmektedir. ödemesinin yapýlmasýný rica ederiz." & vbcrlf & "Saygýlarýmýzla,"

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	Res = Pe1.Write(25, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, 100, imza)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(baslik_baslangic , SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, BilgiGenislik, trim(chadi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, BilgiGenislik, adres1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, BilgiGenislik, adres2)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, BilgiGenislik, adres3)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + SatirYuksekligi*2, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi*4, 180, Mesaj)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, SolMargin ,SatirYuksekligi, 180, imza)

	Call Alanlar()

	Call Baslik(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Satirlar(pe1, i)
	Next
	msgbox dip_tutar
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom + satiryuksekligi, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(dip_tutar))


'	Res = Pe1.Show()
'	Res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

	res = pe1.SetDocExportType(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\YASLANDIR" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & Doc.Saat(6) & ".PDF"
	res = pe1.WriteDoc(dosyaAdi)
	Doc.ShellExecute dosyaAdi
	res = pe1.Close()

	k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Oluþturulan BA-BS 'ler uygun mu?", "Freedom", MB_YESNO)

	Set S = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	if k = 6 Then
		PrintMode = 2 'mail gönderme
		For i = 1 To TL_BORC_ALACAK.GetRecCount()
			If Doc.Is_EY1(TL_BORC_ALACAK.SELECTED(i)) Then
				Call DokumanYarat(pe1,i)
			End If
		Next
	End if


End Sub
Sub Baslik(pe1)
Dim satirpozr
		Degisken = ""
		Sol = solMargin + 8

		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",10)

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			SutunBaslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic , Sol, satiryuksekligi * 2, SutunGenisligi - 2 , SutunBaslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Sub Satirlar(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	Res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",10)
	SatirYuksekligi = 4
		Degisken = ""
		Sol = solMargin + 8
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic + satiryuksekligi * 2
				Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
				End If
dip_tutar = 0
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = DT5.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
				If DegiskenAdi = "_TL" Then
					Degisken = "TL"
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Degisken = Doc.StrToNorDate(Degisken)
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "TUTAR" Then
					dip_tutar = dip_tutar + Degisken
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi <> "VALORGUN" and grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,5)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				End If

				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next

	SatirYuksekligi = 5
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "EVRAKNO"
	Grid(1,0) = "ETN"
	Grid(2,0) = "TARIH"
	Grid(3,0) = "VALORTARIH"
	Grid(4,0) = "CH_TUTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "CH_KUR"
	Grid(6,0) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(7,0) = "_TL"
	Grid(8,0) = "VALORGUN"

	Grid(0,1) = 25
	Grid(1,1) = 35
	Grid(2,1) = 23
	Grid(3,1) = 23
	Grid(4,1) = 23
	Grid(5,1) = 12
	Grid(6,1) = 23
	Grid(7,1) = 8
	Grid(8,1) = 12
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Evrak No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Evrak Türü"
	Grid(2,2) = "Evrak Tarihi"
	Grid(3,2) = "Vade Tarihi"
	Grid(4,2) = "Dövizli Tutar"
	Grid(5,2) = "Döviz Cinsi"
	Grid(6,2) = "TL Tutar"
	Grid(7,2) = ""
	Grid(8,2) = "Valör (Gün)"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "D"
	Grid(3,4) = "D"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = DT5.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,5) = Ondalik		
'			Grid(i,7) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(4,5) = 2
End Sub





Sub MailGonder(AliciMail, dosyaAdi)
	MutabakatMsg = ""
	Call SendMail(AliciMail,"Cari Hesap Mutabakatý", MutabakatMsg, DosyaAdi)
End Sub

Sub SendMail(strTo,strHeader,strMsg,DosyaAdi)
	Kullanici = Doc.Getd7UserName
	Kullanici = Doc.LeftJustify(Kullanici, 8)
	mailserver = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILSERVER", Kullanici, "")
	mailUser = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILKULLANICIADI", Kullanici, "")
	mailPass = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "DATAMODEMNO", Kullanici, "")
	mailFrom = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAIL", Kullanici, "")
	SMTPPort = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "SMTPSERVERPORT", Kullanici, "")
	
	if trim(SMTPPort) = 0 Then SMTPPort = 587

	Set objMessage = CreateObject("CDO.Message")
	objMessage.From = trim(mailFrom)
	objMessage.To = strTo
	objMessage.Subject = strHeader
	objMessage.TextBody = strMsg
	
	if trim(DosyaAdi) <> "" Then
		objMessage.AddAttachment("file://" & DosyaAdi)
	End if
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort 
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = trim(SMTPPort)
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60
	
	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Update
	objMessage.Send
End Sub

Sub VPE_Mail(EMAIL,file_name,Subject)
    Dim pe1
    Dim res

    Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
    res = pe1.Open(1,"h",-1,-1,"")
    res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
    res = pe1.SetMailSubject(Subject)
    res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(Trim(EMAIL),1)
    res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(file_name,0)
    res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)
    res = pe1.Maildoc()
    res = pe1.Close()
End Sub

SET CAPTION="Teklifdffs Fiyat Analizi"

SET DEFAULT_FSTYLE=3

DEFINEFONT ID="default_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8
DEFINEFONT ID="default_text_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_edittext_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_button_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_checkbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 
DEFINEFONT ID="default_groupbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="default_tabcaption_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 

DEFINEFONT ID="text_font01" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

SET DEFAULTFONT=default_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TEXT=default_text_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_EDITTEXT=default_edittext_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_BUTTON=default_button_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_CHECKBOX=default_checkbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_GROUPBOX=default_groupbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TABCAPTION=default_tabcaption_font

DEFINEFONT ID="FONT010" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="FONT011" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 

DEFINEFONT ID="defaultfont_selectedtabcaption" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="defaultfont_unselectedtabcaption" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

SET DEFAULTFONT_SELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_selectedtabcaption
SET DEFAULTFONT_UNSELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_unselectedtabcaption
